"""کاتالوگ ثابت المان‌های صفحه‌ساز معمار.

شناسه هر المان (id) قرارداد عمومی است. قالب‌های فعلی و بعدی فقط از همین
شناسه‌ها استفاده می‌کنند تا مشتری بعداً همان قالب را در معمار ویرایش کند.
شناسه‌ها را عوض نکنید؛ المان تازه فقط با شناسه جدید اضافه می‌شود.
"""


def _f(key, label, kind="text", **extra):
    item = {"key": key, "label": label, "type": kind}
    item.update(extra)
    return item


def _element(element_id, name, group, *, accepts_children=False, settings=None):
    return {
        "id": element_id,
        "name": name,
        "group": group,
        "accepts_children": accepts_children,
        "settings": list(settings or []),
    }


_HEADING = [

]

import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

from apps.architect.catalog import BY_ID
from apps.core.zipsafe import ZipRejected

BUILDER = "memar"
UID_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$")
PAIR_RE = re.compile(
    r"""data-memar-id\s*=\s*["']([a-z0-9-]+)["'][^>]*data-memar-uid\s*=\s*["']([a-z0-9-]+)["']"""
    r"""|data-memar-uid\s*=\s*["']([a-z0-9-]+)["'][^>]*data-memar-id\s*=\s*["']([a-z0-9-]+)["']""",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)
MEMAR_CALL_RE = re.compile(r"""memar\(\s*['"]([a-z0-9-]+)['"]""")
RESERVED_PAGES = {"layout"}


def validate_architect(root: Path, page_slugs: list[str], page_files: dict[str, str]) -> dict:
    path = root / "architect.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise ZipRejected(
            "architect.json در ریشه قالب نیست. هر قالب باید بر اساس صفحه‌ساز معمار باشد. راهنمای زیپ قالب را ببینید."
        )
    try:
        raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise ZipRejected(f"architect.json نامعتبر است: {exc}") from exc
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        raise ZipRejected("architect.json باید یک شیء JSON باشد.")
    if str(raw.get("builder") or "") != BUILDER:
        raise ZipRejected('فیلد builder در architect.json باید دقیقاً "memar" باشد.')
    version = str(raw.get("version") or "").strip()
    if not version:
        raise ZipRejected("فیلد version در architect.json الزامی است.")
    nodes = raw.get("nodes")
    if not isinstance(nodes, list) or not nodes:
        raise ZipRejected("architect.json باید آرایه nodes با حداقل یک المان داشته باشد.")

    allowed_pages = set(page_slugs) | {"layout"}
    normalized = []
    seen = set()
    for index, node in enumerate(nodes, start=1):
        if not isinstance(node, dict):
            raise ZipRejected(f"المان {index} در architect.json شیء نیست.")
        uid = str(node.get("uid") or "").strip()
        element_id = str(node.get("id") or "").strip()
        page = str(node.get("page") or "").strip()
        parent = str(node.get("parent") or "").strip()
        if not UID_RE.fullmatch(uid):
            raise ZipRejected(f"uid نامعتبر است: {uid or index}. فقط حروف کوچک، عدد و خط تیره.")
        if uid in seen:
            raise ZipRejected(f"uid تکراری است: {uid}")
        seen.add(uid)
        if element_id not in BY_ID:
            raise ZipRejected(
                f"شناسه المان معمار ناشناخته است: {element_id or '—'}. فقط ۵۰ شناسه کاتالوگ معمار مجاز است."
            )
        if page not in allowed_pages:
            raise ZipRejected(f"صفحه المان {uid} ناشناخته است: {page}. از slug صفحات یا layout استفاده کنید.")
        settings = node.get("settings") or {}
        if settings is None:
            settings = {}
        if not isinstance(settings, dict):
            raise ZipRejected(f"settings المان {uid} باید شیء باشد.")
        known_keys = {item["key"] for item in BY_ID[element_id]["settings"]}
        unknown = [key for key in settings if key not in known_keys]
        if unknown:
            raise ZipRejected(f"تنظیم ناشناخته برای {element_id}/{uid}: {', '.join(unknown)}")
        clean_settings = {}
        for key, value in settings.items():
            if value is None:
                continue
            clean_settings[key] = str(value)[:4000]
        normalized.append(
            {
                "uid": uid,
                "id": element_id,
                "page": page,
                "parent": parent,
                "settings": clean_settings,
            }
        )

    by_uid = {item["uid"]: item for item in normalized}
    for item in normalized:
        parent = item["parent"]
        if not parent:
            continue
        holder = by_uid.get(parent)
        if holder is None:
            raise ZipRejected(f"والد {parent} برای {item['uid']} در architect.json نیست.")
        if holder["page"] != item["page"]:
            raise ZipRejected(f"المان {item['uid']} و والدش {parent} باید در یک صفحه باشند.")
        if not BY_ID[holder["id"]]["accepts_children"]:
            raise ZipRejected(f"المان {holder['id']} ({parent}) فرزند نمی‌پذیرد.")

    file_pages = _html_pages(root, page_files)
    found = {}
    for page_key, files in file_pages.items():
        for rel, text in files:
            singles = text.count("data-memar-id") + text.count("data-memar-uid")
            pairs = list(PAIR_RE.finditer(text))
            paired_attrs = len(pairs) * 2
            if singles != paired_attrs:
                raise ZipRejected(
                    f"در {rel} هر data-memar-id باید کنار data-memar-uid در همان برچسب باشد."
                )
            for match in pairs:
                if match.group(1):
                    element_id, uid = match.group(1), match.group(2)
                else:
                    uid, element_id = match.group(3), match.group(4)
                node = by_uid.get(uid)
                if node is None:
                    raise ZipRejected(f"uid در HTML هست ولی در architect.json نیست: {uid} ({rel})")
                if node["id"] != element_id:
                    raise ZipRejected(
                        f"شناسه معمار {uid} در HTML برابر {element_id} است ولی در architect.json برابر {node['id']} است."
                    )
                if node["page"] != page_key:
                    raise ZipRejected(f"المان {uid} در فایل صفحه {page_key} است ولی در سند صفحه {node['page']} ثبت شده.")
                found.setdefault(uid, rel)
            for uid in MEMAR_CALL_RE.findall(text):
                if uid not in by_uid:
                    raise ZipRejected(f"فراخوانی memar('{uid}') در {rel} معادلی در architect.json ندارد.")

    missing = [item["uid"] for item in normalized if item["uid"] not in found]
    if missing:
        raise ZipRejected("این uidها در architect.json هستند ولی در HTML با data-memar-uid نیامده‌اند: " + ", ".join(missing))

    pages_with_nodes = {item["page"] for item in normalized}
    for slug in page_slugs:
        if slug in RESERVED_PAGES:
            raise ZipRejected("slug صفحه نباید layout باشد؛ layout برای اسکلت قالب رزرو شده است.")
        if slug not in pages_with_nodes:
            raise ZipRejected(f"صفحه {slug} هیچ المان معمار ندارد.")
        page_file = page_files.get(slug)
        if page_file:
            blob = ""
            for rel, text in file_pages.get(slug, []):
                blob += text
            if "data-memar-uid" not in blob:
                raise ZipRejected(f"فایل صفحه {slug} نشانه‌گذاری معمار ندارد.")

    return {"builder": BUILDER, "version": version, "nodes": normalized}


def load_architect(root: Path) -> dict:
    path = Path(root) / "architect.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        return {"builder": BUILDER, "version": "", "nodes": []}
    try:
        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return {"builder": BUILDER, "version": "", "nodes": []}
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return {"builder": BUILDER, "version": "", "nodes": []}
    nodes = data.get("nodes") if isinstance(data.get("nodes"), list) else []
    return {"builder": str(data.get("builder") or ""), "version": str(data.get("version") or ""), "nodes": nodes}


def _html_pages(root: Path, page_files: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]]:
    by_file = {str(path).replace("\\", "/"): slug for slug, path in page_files.items()}
    grouped: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]] = {}
    for path in sorted(root.rglob("*.html")):
        rel = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        if rel.startswith("layout/"):
            page_key = "layout"
        elif rel in by_file:
            page_key = by_file[rel]
        else:
            page_key = rel
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        grouped.setdefault(page_key, []).append((rel, text))
    return grouped

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\architect\tests.py ===
import io
import json
import shutil
import tempfile
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.test import SimpleTestCase, TestCase, override_settings

from apps.architect.catalog import BY_ID, ELEMENTS
from apps.architect.document import validate_architect
from apps.core.access import build_login_urls
from apps.core.bootstrap_data import ensure_categories, ensure_examples
from apps.core.zipsafe import ZipRejected
from apps.sites.models import Website


class ArchitectCatalogTests(SimpleTestCase):
    def test_catalog_has_fifty_stable_ids(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(ELEMENTS), 50)
        self.assertEqual(len(BY_ID), 50)
        for item in ELEMENTS:
            self.assertEqual(item["id"], item["id"].lower())
            self.assertNotIn(" ", item["id"])

    def test_login_urls_are_real_browser_addresses(self):
        urls = build_login_urls(
            ["203.0.113.10"],
            "8787",
            "secret-pass",
            [{"domain": "panel.example.com", "https": True}, {"domain": "panel.example.net", "https": False}],
        )
        self.assertEqual(urls[0], "http://203.0.113.10:8787/secret-pass/")
        self.assertIn("http://203.0.113.10/secret-pass/", urls)
        self.assertIn("https://panel.example.com/secret-pass/", urls)
        self.assertIn("http://panel.example.net/secret-pass/", urls)
        self.assertIn("http://panel.example.net:8787/secret-pass/", urls)
        blob = " ".join(urls)
        self.assertNotIn("SERVER", blob)
        self.assertNotIn("IP-SERVER", blob)

    def test_login_urls_skip_placeholder_hosts(self):
        self.assertEqual(build_login_urls(["127.0.0.1", "SERVER", ""], "8787", "panel", []), [])

    def test_docs_name_every_element_id(self):
        guide = Path(settings.BASE_DIR, "docs", "template-zip-guide.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for item in ELEMENTS:
            self.assertIn(item["id"], guide)
        self.assertIn("architect.json", guide)
        self.assertIn("memar(", guide)

    def test_sample_templates_match_architect_rules(self):
        samples = {
            "jewelry-atelier": ["home", "collection", "about", "contact"],
            "silver-phones": ["home", "phones", "about", "contact"],
        }
        for slug, pages in samples.items():
            root = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "examples" / "templates" / slug
            files = {page: f"pages/{page}.html" for page in pages}
            document = validate_architect(root, pages, files)
            self.assertEqual(document["builder"], "memar")
            self.assertGreaterEqual(len(document["nodes"]), 10)

    def test_rejects_unknown_element(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            bare = Path(tmp)
            (bare / "pages").mkdir()
            (bare / "pages" / "home.html").write_text("<h1>سلام</h1>", encoding="utf-8")
            with self.assertRaises(ZipRejected):
                validate_architect(bare, ["home"], {"home": "pages/home.html"})
            (bare / "architect.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(
                    {
                        "builder": "memar",
                        "version": "1.0",
                        "nodes": [{"uid": "home-title", "id": "not-a-widget", "page": "home", "settings": {}}],
                    }
                ),
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            with self.assertRaises(ZipRejected) as caught:
                validate_architect(bare, ["home"], {"home": "pages/home.html"})
            self.assertIn("ناشناخته", str(caught.exception))


class ArchitectZipBytesTests(SimpleTestCase):
    def test_sample_zips_keep_architect_file(self):
        root = Path(settings.BASE_DIR) / "examples" / "templates" / "silver-phones"
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, "w") as archive:
            for path in root.rglob("*"):
                if path.is_file():
                    archive.write(path, path.relative_to(root).as_posix())
        buf.seek(0)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(buf) as archive:
            self.assertIn("architect.json", set(archive.namelist()))


@override_settings(MEDIA_ROOT=tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mohsen-memar-"))
class ArchitectRenderTests(TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        shutil.rmtree(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, ignore_errors=True)
        super().tearDownClass()

    def setUp(self):
        ensure_categories()
        notes = ensure_examples()
        self.notes = notes
        get_user_model().objects.create_superuser("admin", "admin@example.com", "pass12345")

    def test_customer_edit_changes_live_text(self):
        self.assertFalse(any("نامعتبر" in note or "architect" in note for note in self.notes), self.notes)
        site = Website.objects.get(domain="zarrin.local")
        site.settings = {"memar": {"home-title": {"text": "عنوان ویرایش‌شده معمار"}}}
        site.save(update_fields=["settings"])
        response = self.client.get("/", HTTP_HOST="zarrin.local")
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "عنوان ویرایش‌شده معمار")
        self.assertContains(response, 'data-memar-id="heading"')
        self.assertContains(response, 'data-memar-uid="home-title"')

    def test_panel_editor_and_guide(self):
        self.client.login(username="admin", password="pass12345")
        site = Website.objects.get(domain="hamrah.local")
        response = self.client.get(f"/panel/sites/{site.pk}/memar/")
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "معمار")
        self.assertContains(response, "home-title")
        self.assertContains(response, "heading")
        guide = self.client.get("/panel/guide/template/")
        self.assertContains(guide, "architect.json")
        self.assertContains(guide, "product-grid")
        self.assertContains(guide, "contact-form")

from django.contrib import messages
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render

from apps.architect.catalog import BY_ID, GROUPS
from apps.architect.document import load_architect
from apps.core.audit import log_action
from apps.core.decorators import staff_required
from apps.sites.models import Website


@staff_required
def site_memar(request, pk):
    website = get_object_or_404(Website, pk=pk)
    root = website.template.extracted_path
    document = load_architect(root)
    nodes = [item for item in document.get("nodes") or [] if isinstance(item, dict)]
    if request.method == "POST":
        saved = {}
        for node in nodes:
            uid = str(node.get("uid") or "")
            spec = BY_ID.get(str(node.get("id") or ""))
            if not uid or spec is None:
                continue
            bucket = {}
            for field in spec["settings"]:
                key = field["key"]
                raw = request.POST.get(f"{uid}__{key}")
                if raw is None:
                    continue
                value = str(raw).strip()
                if value:
                    bucket[key] = value[:4000]
            if bucket:
                saved[uid] = bucket
        settings = dict(website.settings or {})
        settings["memar"] = saved
        website.settings = settings
        website.save(update_fields=["settings", "updated_at"])
        log_action(request, "site.memar", website.domain, f"{len(saved)} المان")
        messages.success(request, "ویرایش معمار ذخیره شد و روی سایت مشتری اعمال می‌شود.")
        return redirect("site-memar", pk=pk)

    overrides = {}
    raw_over = (website.settings or {}).get("memar") or {}
    if isinstance(raw_over, dict):
        overrides = raw_over
    pages = _pages(website, nodes, overrides)
    return render(
        request,
        "panel/sites/memar.html",
        {
            "obj": website,
            "pages": pages,
            "groups": GROUPS,
            "node_count": len(nodes),
            "ready": bool(nodes) and document.get("builder") == "memar",
        },
    )


def _pages(website, nodes, overrides):
    titles = {"layout": "اسکلت قالب"}
    for page in (website.template.manifest or {}).get("pages") or []:
        titles[page.get("slug")] = page.get("title") or page.get("slug")
    order = []
    buckets = {}
    for node in nodes:
        uid = str(node.get("uid") or "")
        element_id = str(node.get("id") or "")
        spec = BY_ID.get(element_id)
        if spec is None or not uid:
            continue
        page = str(node.get("page") or "layout")
        if page not in buckets:
            buckets[page] = []
            order.append(page)
        current = overrides.get(uid) if isinstance(overrides.get(uid), dict) else {}
        fields = []
        for field in spec["settings"]:
            fields.append(
                {
                    "key": field["key"],
                    "label": field["label"],
                    "type": field["type"],
                    "options": field.get("options") or [],
                    "value": current.get(field["key"], ""),
                }
            )
        buckets[page].append(
            {
                "uid": uid,
                "id": element_id,
                "name": spec["name"],
                "group": spec["group"],
                "parent": node.get("parent") or "",
                "fields": fields,
            }
        )
    return [{"slug": slug, "title": titles.get(slug) or slug, "nodes": buckets[slug]} for slug in order]

import re

from django import forms
from django.utils.text import slugify

from apps.categories.models import Category

ICON_CHOICES = [
    ("gem", "جواهر"),
    ("crown", "تاج"),
    ("device", "دستگاه"),
    ("shop", "فروشگاه"),
    ("star", "ستاره"),
    ("leaf", "برگ"),
]


class CategoryForm(forms.ModelForm):
    slug = forms.CharField(label="نامک", required=False)
    color = forms.CharField(label="رنگ", widget=forms.TextInput(attrs={"type": "color"}))
    icon = forms.ChoiceField(label="نماد", choices=ICON_CHOICES)

    class Meta:
        model = Category
        fields = ["name", "slug", "tier_label", "description", "color", "icon", "sort_order", "is_active"]
        widgets = {"description": forms.Textarea(attrs={"rows": 4})}

    def clean_color(self):
        color = (self.cleaned_data.get("color") or "").strip()
        if not re.fullmatch(r"#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}", color):
            raise forms.ValidationError("رنگ باید مثل #C6A15B باشد.")
        return color.upper()

    def clean_slug(self):
        raw = (self.cleaned_data.get("slug") or "").strip()
        source = raw or self.cleaned_data.get("name") or "category"
        slug = slugify(source, allow_unicode=True)[:80]
        if not slug:
            slug = "category"
        qs = Category.objects.filter(slug=slug)
        if self.instance.pk:
            qs = qs.exclude(pk=self.instance.pk)
        if qs.exists():
            raise forms.ValidationError("این نامک قبلاً استفاده شده است.")
        return slug

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\categories\models.py ===
from django.db import models


class Category(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField("نام", max_length=120)
    slug = models.SlugField("نامک", max_length=80, unique=True, allow_unicode=True)
    tier_label = models.CharField("برچسب سطح", max_length=80, blank=True)
    description = models.TextField("توضیح", blank=True)
    color = models.CharField("رنگ", max_length=7, default="#B6894A")
    icon = models.CharField("نماد", max_length=40, default="gem")
    sort_order = models.PositiveIntegerField("ترتیب", default=0)
    is_active = models.BooleanField("فعال", default=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["sort_order", "name"]
        verbose_name = "دسته"
        verbose_name_plural = "دسته‌ها"

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name

    @property
    def site_count(self):
        return self.websites.count()

    @property
    def template_count(self):
        return self.templates.count()

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\categories\views.py ===
from django.contrib import messages
from django.db.models import Count
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render

from apps.categories.forms import CategoryForm
from apps.categories.models import Category
from apps.core.audit import log_action
from apps.core.decorators import staff_required
from apps.plugins.models import CategoryPlugin, PluginPackage


@staff_required
def category_list(request):
    q = (request.GET.get("q") or "").strip()
    rows = Category.objects.annotate(
        n_sites=Count("websites", distinct=True),
        n_templates=Count("templates", distinct=True),
        n_plugins=Count("category_plugins", distinct=True),
    )
    if q:
        rows = rows.filter(name__icontains=q)
    return render(request, "panel/categories/list.html", {"rows": rows, "q": q})


@staff_required
def category_create(request):
    form = CategoryForm(request.POST or None)
    if request.method == "POST" and form.is_valid():
        obj = form.save()
        log_action(request, "category.create", obj.name, obj.slug)
        messages.success(request, "دسته ساخته شد.")
        return redirect("category-list")
    return render(request, "panel/categories/form.html", {"form": form, "mode": "create"})


@staff_required
def category_edit(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(Category, pk=pk)
    form = CategoryForm(request.POST or None, instance=obj)
    plugins = PluginPackage.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    assigned = set(obj.category_plugins.filter(enabled=True).values_list("plugin_id", flat=True))
    if request.method == "POST" and request.POST.get("form") == "plugins":
        chosen = {int(x) for x in request.POST.getlist("plugins") if x.isdigit()}
        for plugin in plugins:
            if plugin.id in chosen:
                CategoryPlugin.objects.update_or_create(
                    category=obj, plugin=plugin, defaults={"enabled": True}
                )
            else:
                CategoryPlugin.objects.filter(category=obj, plugin=plugin).delete()
        log_action(request, "category.plugins", obj.name, f"{len(chosen)} افزونه")
        messages.success(request, "افزونه‌های دسته ذخیره شد. همه سایت‌های این دسته همان افزونه‌ها را دارند.")
        return redirect("category-edit", pk=obj.pk)
    if request.method == "POST" and form.is_valid():
        form.save()
        log_action(request, "category.update", obj.name, obj.slug)
        messages.success(request, "دسته به‌روز شد.")
        return redirect("category-list")
    return render(
        request,
        "panel/categories/form.html",
        {"form": form, "mode": "edit", "obj": obj, "plugins": plugins, "assigned": assigned},
    )


@staff_required
def category_delete(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(Category, pk=pk)
    if request.method == "POST":
        if obj.websites.exists() or obj.templates.exists():
            messages.error(request, "دسته دارای سایت یا قالب است و حذف نمی‌شود.")
            return redirect("category-edit", pk=pk)
        name = obj.name
        obj.delete()
        log_action(request, "category.delete", name, "")
        messages.success(request, "دسته حذف شد.")
        return redirect("category-list")
    return render(request, "panel/confirm_delete.html", {"object_name": obj.name, "cancel": "category-list"})

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand

from apps.core.bootstrap_data import ensure_admin_user, ensure_categories, ensure_examples


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "دسته‌ها، قالب‌ها و افزونه‌های نمونه را نصب می‌کند."

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        created = ensure_categories()
        notes = ensure_examples()
        user, user_created = ensure_admin_user()
        if created:
            self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS("دسته‌های تازه: " + ", ".join(created)))
        for line in notes:
            self.stdout.write(line)
        if user_created and user is not None:
            self.stdout.write(self.style.WARNING(f"کاربر مدیر ساخته شد: {user.username}"))
            if user.username == "admin":
                self.stdout.write(self.style.WARNING("رمز توسعه: admin12345  — فقط برای محیط توسعه."))
        self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS("bootstrap تمام شد."))

import os

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError

from apps.core.bootstrap_data import ensure_admin_user


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "کاربر مدیر را از متغیرهای محیط می‌سازد یا رمزش را بازنشانی می‌کند."

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        if not os.environ.get("MOHSEN_ADMIN_USER") or not os.environ.get("MOHSEN_ADMIN_PASSWORD"):
            raise CommandError("MOHSEN_ADMIN_USER و MOHSEN_ADMIN_PASSWORD لازم است.")
        user, created = ensure_admin_user()
        state = "ساخته شد" if created else "به‌روز شد"
        self.stdout.write(self.style.SUCCESS(f"کاربر {user.username} {state}."))

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\core\templatetags\panel_tags.py ===
import jdatetime
from django import template
from django.utils import timezone

register = template.Library()


@register.filter
def jalali(value):
    if not value:
        return ""
    try:
        local = timezone.localtime(value) if timezone.is_aware(value) else value
        converted = jdatetime.datetime.fromgregorian(datetime=local.replace(tzinfo=None))
        return converted.strftime("%Y/%m/%d %H:%M")
    except Exception:
        return str(value)


@register.filter
def get_item(mapping, key):
    if isinstance(mapping, dict):
        return mapping.get(key)
    return None

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\core\access.py ===
import os
import re
import socket
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

CRED_PATH = Path("/root/mohsen-credentials.txt")
IPV4_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}$")
USER_RE = re.compile(r"(?:نام کاربری|USERNAME)\s*[:=]\s*(\S+)", re.IGNORECASE)
PASS_RE = re.compile(r"(?:رمز عبور|PASSWORD)\s*[:=]\s*(\S+)", re.IGNORECASE)


def _valid_public_ip(value: str) -> bool:
    if not IPV4_RE.fullmatch(value or ""):
        return False
    parts = [int(part) for part in value.split(".")]
    if any(part > 255 for part in parts):
        return False
    if value.startswith("127.") or value == "0.0.0.0" or value.startswith("169.254."):
        return False
    return True


def server_ips() -> list[str]:
    found: list[str] = []

    def add(raw: str) -> None:
        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", raw or ""):
            token = token.strip().split("%", 1)[0]
            if _valid_public_ip(token) and token not in found:
                found.append(token)

    try:
        result = subprocess.run(["hostname", "-I"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3)
        add(result.stdout or "")
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        pass
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(["ip", "-4", "-o", "addr", "show", "scope", "global"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3)
        add(" ".join(re.findall(r"inet\s+(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)", result.stdout or "")))
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        pass
    try:
        sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        sock.settimeout(1)
        sock.connect(("8.8.8.8", 80))
        add(sock.getsockname()[0])
        sock.close()
    except OSError:
        pass
    return found


def build_login_urls(ips, port, web_pass, domains) -> list[str]:
    """آدرس‌هایی که واقعاً در نوار مرورگر باز می‌شوند. میزبان ساختگی برنمی‌گرداند."""
    port = str(port or "8787").strip()
    web_pass = str(web_pass or "").strip().strip("/")
    if not web_pass or not port.isdigit():
        return []
    urls: list[str] = []
    seen = set()

    def push(url: str) -> None:
        if not url or url in seen:
            return
        if any(token in url for token in ("SERVER", "IP-SERVER", "SERVER_IP", "example.invalid")):
            return
        seen.add(url)
        urls.append(url)

    for ip in ips or []:
        if not _valid_public_ip(str(ip)):
            continue
        push(f"http://{ip}:{port}/{web_pass}/")
        push(f"http://{ip}/{web_pass}/")
    for row in domains or []:
        if isinstance(row, dict):
            domain = str(row.get("domain") or "").strip().strip(".")
            https = bool(row.get("https"))
        else:
            domain = str(getattr(row, "domain", "") or "").strip().strip(".")
            https = bool(getattr(row, "https", False))
        if not domain or " " in domain or domain.upper() in {"SERVER", "SERVER_IP", "IP-SERVER"}:
            continue
        if https:
            push(f"https://{domain}/{web_pass}/")
        else:
            push(f"http://{domain}/{web_pass}/")
            if port not in {"80", "443"}:
                push(f"http://{domain}:{port}/{web_pass}/")
    return urls


def read_credentials() -> dict:
    username = os.environ.get("MOHSEN_ADMIN_USER", "").strip() or "admin"
    password = ""
    path = CRED_PATH if CRED_PATH.is_file() else None
    if path is not None:
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        user_match = USER_RE.search(text)
        pass_match = PASS_RE.search(text)
        if user_match:
            username = user_match.group(1)
        if pass_match:
            password = pass_match.group(1)
    return {"username": username, "password": password, "path": path}


def login_snapshot() -> dict:
    from django.conf import settings

    from apps.core.models import AdminDomain

    domains = []
    try:
        domains = list(AdminDomain.objects.all())
    except Exception:
        domains = []
    urls = build_login_urls(server_ips(), settings.PANEL_PORT, settings.WEB_PASS, domains)
    creds = read_credentials()
    return {
        "urls": urls,
        "username": creds["username"],
        "password": creds["password"],
        "credentials_path": creds["path"],
        "port": str(settings.PANEL_PORT),
        "web_pass": settings.WEB_PASS,
    }


def write_credentials(username: str, password: str, urls: list[str]) -> None:
    if not username or not password:
        return
    lines = [
        "محسن — مشخصات ورود",
        "این آدرس را عیناً در نوار مرورگر وارد کنید:",
    ]
    for index, url in enumerate(urls, start=1):
        lines.append(f"LOGIN_URL_{index}={url}")
    lines.append(f"USERNAME={username}")
    lines.append(f"PASSWORD={password}")
    lines.append(f"نام کاربری: {username}")
    lines.append(f"رمز عبور: {password}")
    lines.append("دستور مدیریت: sudo mohsen")
    lines.append("به‌روزرسانی از گیت‌هاب: sudo mohsen update")
    lines.append("حذف کامل: sudo mohsen uninstall --yes")
    try:
        CRED_PATH.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        os.chmod(CRED_PATH, 0o600)
    except OSError:
        return


def sync_credentials(password: str | None = None, username: str | None = None) -> None:
    current = read_credentials()
    user = username or current["username"]
    secret = password if password is not None else current["password"]
    if not secret:
        return
    snap = login_snapshot()
    write_credentials(user, secret, snap["urls"])

def log_action(request, action, obj="", message=""):
    from apps.core.models import ActivityLog

    user = getattr(request, "user", None)
    username = ""
    if user is not None and getattr(user, "is_authenticated", False):
        username = user.get_username()
    ActivityLog.objects.create(
        username=username[:150],
        action=str(action)[:80],
        object_repr=str(obj)[:200],
        message=str(message)[:500],
        ip=(request.META.get("REMOTE_ADDR") or "")[:64],
    )

import io
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model

from apps.categories.models import Category
from apps.core.zipsafe import ZipRejected
from apps.plugins.models import CategoryPlugin, PluginPackage
from apps.plugins.services import store_plugin
from apps.sites.models import Website
from apps.templates_engine.models import TemplatePackage
from apps.templates_engine.services import store_template


def zip_tree(root: Path) -> bytes:
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        for path in root.rglob("*"):
            if not path.is_file():
                continue
            if "__pycache__" in path.parts or path.suffix == ".pyc":
                continue
            zf.write(path, path.relative_to(root).as_posix())
    return buf.getvalue()


def ensure_categories():
    specs = [
        {
            "slug": "gold",
            "name": "طلایی",
            "tier_label": "Gold",
            "description": "سطح تجاری لوکس. مناسب فروشگاه‌هایی که قالب و افزونه اختصاصی سطح بالا می‌خواهند.",
            "color": "#B6894A",
            "icon": "gem",
            "sort_order": 1,
        },
        {
            "slug": "silver",
            "name": "نقره‌ای",
            "tier_label": "Silver",
            "description": "سطح فروشگاهی. مناسب کسب‌وکارهایی که صفحه عمومی و ابزار ساخت صفحه می‌خواهند.",
            "color": "#8D99A6",
            "icon": "device",
            "sort_order": 2,
        },
    ]
    created = []
    for spec in specs:
        obj, was_created = Category.objects.get_or_create(slug=spec["slug"], defaults=spec)
        if was_created:
            created.append(obj.slug)
    return created


def ensure_examples():
    base = Path(settings.BASE_DIR)
    notes = []
    gold = Category.objects.filter(slug="gold").first()
    silver = Category.objects.filter(slug="silver").first()
    pairs = [
        (base / "examples" / "templates" / "jewelry-atelier", gold, "jewelry-atelier"),
        (base / "examples" / "templates" / "silver-phones", silver, "silver-phones"),
    ]
    for folder, category, slug in pairs:
        if category is None or not folder.is_dir():
            continue
        if TemplatePackage.objects.filter(slug=slug).exists():
            notes.append(f"قالب {slug} از قبل نصب است")
            continue
        try:
            obj, created = store_template(raw=zip_tree(folder), category=category)
        except ZipRejected as exc:
            notes.append(f"قالب {folder.name}: {exc}")
            continue
        notes.append(f"قالب {obj.slug} نصب شد" if created else f"قالب {obj.slug} به‌روز شد")
    plugin_pairs = [
        (base / "examples" / "plugins" / "sms", "sms", gold),
        (base / "examples" / "plugins" / "page-builder", "page-builder", silver),
    ]
    for folder, slug, category in plugin_pairs:
        if not folder.is_dir():
            continue
        obj = PluginPackage.objects.filter(slug=slug).first()
        if obj is None:
            try:
                obj, created = store_plugin(raw=zip_tree(folder))
            except ZipRejected as exc:
                notes.append(f"افزونه {slug}: {exc}")
                continue
            notes.append(f"افزونه {obj.slug} نصب شد" if created else f"افزونه {obj.slug} به‌روز شد")
        else:
            notes.append(f"افزونه {slug} از قبل نصب است")
        if category is not None and obj is not None:
            CategoryPlugin.objects.get_or_create(
                category=category, plugin=obj, defaults={"enabled": True, "settings": {}}
            )
    _ensure_sample_sites()
    return notes

            phone="021-91098080",

            notes="نمونه نصب",

def ensure_admin_user():
    import os

    username = os.environ.get("MOHSEN_ADMIN_USER", "").strip()
    password = os.environ.get("MOHSEN_ADMIN_PASSWORD", "").strip()
    User = get_user_model()
    if username and password:
        user, created = User.objects.get_or_create(username=username, defaults={"is_staff": True, "is_superuser": True, "email": ""})
        if created or os.environ.get("MOHSEN_ADMIN_RESET", "") == "1":
            user.is_staff = True
            user.is_superuser = True
            user.set_password(password)
            user.save()
        return user, created
    if settings.DEBUG and not User.objects.filter(is_staff=True).exists():
        user = User(username="admin", email="admin@localhost", is_staff=True, is_superuser=True)
        user.set_password("admin12345")
        user.save()
        return user, True
    return None, False

import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from apps.core.access import login_snapshot, sync_credentials


def bootstrap_django():
    from apps.core.envfile import load_env_into_os

    load_env_into_os()
    os.environ.setdefault("DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE", "config.settings.prod")
    if not os.environ.get("MOHSEN_SECRET_KEY"):
        os.environ["DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE"] = "config.settings.dev"
    import django

    django.setup()


def _hr():
    print("─" * 64)


def _banner():
    print()
    print("╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗")
    print("║              محسن  ·  مدیریت سایت‌ساز               ║")
    print("╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝")


def _root():
    return hasattr(os, "geteuid") and os.geteuid() == 0


def _require_root():
    if not _root():
        print("این کار به root نیاز دارد. دستور را این‌طور اجرا کنید:  sudo mohsen")
        return False
    return True


def _print_login(snapshot=None):
    snap = snapshot or login_snapshot()
    _hr()
    print("ورود پنل — این آدرس را عیناً در نوار مرورگر وارد کنید")
    if snap["urls"]:
        for url in snap["urls"]:
            print(f"  {url}")
    else:
        print("  آی‌پی قابل استفاده روی کارت شبکه پیدا نشد.")
        print("  آدرس ساختگی نشان داده نمی‌شود، چون در مرورگر باز نمی‌شود.")
        print("  روی سرور بررسی کنید: hostname -I")
    print(f"  نام کاربری: {snap['username']}")
    if snap["password"] and _root():
        print(f"  رمز عبور:   {snap['password']}")
    elif snap["password"]:
        print("  رمز عبور:   (برای دیدن رمز: sudo mohsen)")
    elif os.environ.get("DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE", "").endswith(".dev"):
        print("  رمز عبور:   در توسعه محلی، اگر bootstrap شده باشد admin12345 است.")
    else:
        print("  رمز عبور:   در /root/mohsen-credentials.txt پیدا نشد.")
    if snap["credentials_path"]:
        print(f"  فایل رمز:   {snap['credentials_path']}")
    _hr()


def _menu():
    _banner()
    _print_login()
    print("  1) وضعیت سیستم")
    print("  2) افزودن دامنه پنل (add domain)")
    print("  3) نمایش دوباره اطلاعات ورود")

    print(" 10) حذف کامل پروژه         sudo mohsen uninstall --yes")
    print(" 11) صدور و تمدید TLS       sudo mohsen tls")
    print("  0) خروج")
    _hr()


def _project_root() -> Path:
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def status():
    from django.conf import settings

    from apps.categories.models import Category
    from apps.plugins.models import PluginPackage
    from apps.sites.models import Website

    _banner()
    active = "نامشخص"
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(["systemctl", "is-active", "mohsen"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
        active = (result.stdout or result.stderr or "").strip() or "نامشخص"
    except Exception:
        active = "systemd در دسترس نیست"
    db_path = Path(settings.DATABASES["default"].get("NAME", ""))
    size = ""
    if db_path.is_file():
        size = f"{db_path.stat().st_size / 1024:.0f} KB"
    snap = login_snapshot()
    print(f"  نسخه:          {settings.VERSION}")
    print(f"  سرویس:         {active}")
    print(f"  پورت پنل:      {settings.PANEL_PORT}")
    print(f"  مسیر محرمانه:  /{settings.WEB_PASS}/")
    print(f"  پایگاه داده:   {db_path} {size}")
    print(f"  دسته‌ها:       {Category.objects.count()}")
    print(f"  قالب‌ها:       {__import__('apps.templates_engine.models', fromlist=['TemplatePackage']).TemplatePackage.objects.count()}")
    print(f"  افزونه‌ها:     {PluginPackage.objects.count()}")
    print(f"  وب‌سایت‌ها:    {Website.objects.count()}")
    try:
        from apps.tls.models import Certificate

        print(f"  گواهی فعال:    {Certificate.objects.filter(status=Certificate.Status.ACTIVE).count()}")
        print(f"  گواهی ناموفق:  {Certificate.objects.filter(status=Certificate.Status.FAILED).count()}")
    except Exception:
        print("  گواهی TLS:     هنوز آماده نیست")
    _print_login(snap)


def show_login():
    _banner()
    _print_login()


def add_domain():
    from django.conf import settings

    from apps.core.models import AdminDomain
    from apps.sites.domains import validate_admin_domain

    if not _require_root():
        return
    print("دامنه‌ای که کنار آی‌پی، پنل را باز کند. مثال: panel.example.com")
    raw = input("دامنه: ").strip()
    try:
        domain = validate_admin_domain(raw)
    except ValueError as exc:
        print(exc)
        return
    AdminDomain.objects.filter(is_primary=True).update(is_primary=False)
    AdminDomain.objects.update_or_create(domain=domain, defaults={"is_primary": True, "note": "افزوده‌شده با دستور mohsen"})
    registry = Path("/etc/mohsen/admin-domains.txt")
    try:
        registry.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        names = "\n".join(AdminDomain.objects.values_list("domain", flat=True))
        registry.write_text(names + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError as exc:
        print(f"هشدار: فهرست دامنه نوشته نشد: {exc}")
    from apps.tls.names import is_public_dns_name
    from apps.tls.service import enqueue

    print()
    print("دامنه ثبت شد. رکورد DNS از نوع A را به آی‌پی همین سرور اشاره دهید.")
    if is_public_dns_name(domain):
        print(enqueue(domain, "panel", force=False))
    else:
        print("برای آی‌پی یا نام محلی گواهی عمومی صادر نمی‌شود.")
        print(f"  ورود با پورت پنل: http://{domain}:{settings.PANEL_PORT}/{settings.WEB_PASS}/")
    _print_login()
    sync_credentials()
    _hr()


def remove_domain():
    from apps.core.models import AdminDomain

    if not _require_root():
        return
    rows = list(AdminDomain.objects.all())
    if not rows:
        print("دامنه‌ای ثبت نشده است.")
        return
    for index, row in enumerate(rows, start=1):
        print(f"  {index}) {row.domain}")
    raw = input("شماره دامنه برای حذف: ").strip()
    if not raw.isdigit() or not (1 <= int(raw) <= len(rows)):
        print("شماره نامعتبر است.")
        return
    row = rows[int(raw) - 1]
    from apps.tls.service import enqueue_remove

    print(enqueue_remove(row.domain))
    row.delete()
    print(f"{row.domain} حذف شد.")
    _print_login()
    sync_credentials()


def change_password():
    from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model

    if not _require_root():
        return
    User = get_user_model()
    users = list(User.objects.filter(is_staff=True))
    if not users:
        print("کاربر مدیری وجود ندارد.")
        return
    for index, user in enumerate(users, start=1):
        print(f"  {index}) {user.username}")
    raw = input("شماره کاربر [1]: ").strip() or "1"
    if not raw.isdigit() or not (1 <= int(raw) <= len(users)):
        print("شماره نامعتبر است.")
        return
    user = users[int(raw) - 1]
    password = input("رمز جدید (حداقل ۸ نویسه): ").strip()
    again = input("تکرار رمز: ").strip()
    if len(password) < 8 or password != again:
        print("رمز معتبر نیست یا تکرارش یکی نیست.")
        return
    user.set_password(password)
    user.save()
    sync_credentials(password=password, username=user.username)
    print(f"رمز {user.username} عوض شد و در /root/mohsen-credentials.txt نوشته شد.")
    _print_login()


def change_web_pass():
    import secrets

    if not _require_root():
        return
    suggested = secrets.token_urlsafe(9)
    raw = input(f"مسیر محرمانه جدید [{suggested}]: ").strip() or suggested
    raw = raw.strip().strip("/")
    if not raw or "/" in raw or len(raw) < 6:
        print("مسیر باید بدون اسلش و حداقل ۶ نویسه باشد.")
        return
    from apps.core.envfile import update_keys

    try:
        files = update_keys({"MOHSEN_WEB_PASS": raw})
    except Exception as exc:
        print(exc)
        return
    os.environ["MOHSEN_WEB_PASS"] = raw
    from django.conf import settings

    settings.WEB_PASS = raw
    print("فایل‌های به‌روز شده:")
    for item in files:
        print(" ", item)
    sync_credentials()
    print("آدرس تازه:")
    _print_login()
    answer = input("سرویس الان راه‌اندازی مجدد شود؟ [Y/n]: ").strip().lower()
    if answer not in {"n", "no", "خیر"}:
        restart_service()


def restart_service():
    if not _require_root():
        return
    proc = subprocess.run(["systemctl", "restart", "mohsen"])
    if proc.returncode == 0:
        print("سرویس mohsen راه‌اندازی مجدد شد.")
    else:
        print("راه‌اندازی مجدد ناموفق بود. وضعیت: systemctl status mohsen")


def backup():
    from django.conf import settings

    if not _require_root():
        return
    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    dest = Path("/var/backups/mohsen") / stamp
    dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    db = Path(settings.DATABASES["default"].get("NAME", ""))
    if db.is_file():
        shutil.copy2(db, dest / db.name)
    media = Path(settings.MEDIA_ROOT)
    if media.is_dir():
        shutil.copytree(media, dest / "media", dirs_exist_ok=True)
    env = Path("/etc/mohsen/mohsen.env")
    if env.is_file():
        shutil.copy2(env, dest / "mohsen.env")
    ssl = Path("/etc/mohsen/ssl")
    if ssl.is_dir():
        shutil.copytree(ssl, dest / "ssl", dirs_exist_ok=True)
    os.chmod(dest, 0o700)
    print(f"پشتیبان ساخته شد: {dest}")


def update_project():
    if not _require_root():
        return
    script = _project_root() / "deploy" / "update.sh"
    if not script.is_file():
        print("فایل به‌روزرسانی پیدا نشد: deploy/update.sh")
        return
    print("دریافت کد تازه از https://github.com/mtma6991-arch/mohsen")
    os.execv("/bin/bash", ["bash", str(script)])


def uninstall_project():
    if not _require_root():
        return
    print("حذف کامل محسن: سرویس، سایت nginx، دستور، کد، پایگاه، رسانه، تنظیمات و فایل رمز.")
    print("برگشت‌پذیر نیست. برای تأیید بنویسید: uninstall")
    answer = input("تأیید: ").strip().lower()
    if answer != "uninstall":
        print("لغو شد.")
        return
    script = _project_root() / "uninstall.sh"
    os.execv("/bin/bash", ["bash", str(script), "--yes"])


def tls_maintain_cli():
    if not _require_root():
        return
    from apps.tls.service import perform_maintain

    print("صدور گواهی‌های جاافتاده و تمدید نزدیک به انقضا. ممکن است چند دقیقه طول بکشد.")
    print(perform_maintain())


def main(argv=None):
    argv = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
    if argv and argv[0] == "update":
        if not _require_root():
            return 1
        script = _project_root() / "deploy" / "update.sh"
        os.execv("/bin/bash", ["bash", str(script)])
    if argv and argv[0] == "uninstall":
        if not _require_root():
            return 1
        script = _project_root() / "uninstall.sh"
        os.execv("/bin/bash", ["bash", str(script), *argv[1:]])
    try:
        bootstrap_django()
    except Exception as exc:
        print("برنامه بالا نیامد. اگر روی سرور هستید فایل /etc/mohsen/mohsen.env را بررسی کنید.")
        print(exc)
        return 1
    actions = {
        "1": status,
        "2": add_domain,
        "add-domain": add_domain,
        "3": show_login,
        "login": show_login,
        "4": change_password,
        "5": change_web_pass,
        "6": restart_service,
        "7": backup,
        "8": remove_domain,
        "9": update_project,
        "update": update_project,
        "10": uninstall_project,
        "uninstall": uninstall_project,
        "11": tls_maintain_cli,
        "tls": tls_maintain_cli,
        "status": status,
    }
    if argv:
        action = actions.get(argv[0])
        if action is None:
            print("فرمان ناشناخته.")
            print("  sudo mohsen")
            print("  sudo mohsen tls")
            print("  sudo mohsen update")
            print("  sudo mohsen uninstall --yes")
            return 2
        action()
        return 0
    while True:
        _menu()
        choice = input("گزینه: ").strip().lower()
        if choice in {"0", "q", "exit", "خروج"}:
            print("خداحافظ.")
            return 0
        action = actions.get(choice)
        if action is None:
            print("گزینه نامعتبر است.")
            continue
        try:
            action()
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print("\nلغو شد.")
        except EOFError:
            print()
            return 0
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"خطا: {exc}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\core\context_processors.py ===
from django.conf import settings

from apps.core.models import AdminDomain


def panel(request):
    prefix = f"/{settings.WEB_PASS}/"
    if not request.path.startswith(prefix):
        return {}
    domains = list(AdminDomain.objects.all()[:20])
    return {
        "WEB_PASS": settings.WEB_PASS,
        "PANEL_PORT": settings.PANEL_PORT,
        "MOHSEN_VERSION": settings.VERSION,
        "admin_domains": domains,
        "panel_prefix": prefix,
    }

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\core\decorators.py ===
from functools import wraps

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.auth.views import redirect_to_login
from django.shortcuts import redirect


def staff_required(view):
    @wraps(view)
    def _wrapped(request, *args, **kwargs):
        user = request.user
        if not user.is_authenticated:
            return redirect_to_login(request.get_full_path(), settings.LOGIN_URL)
        if not user.is_staff:
            return redirect(settings.LOGIN_URL)
        return view(request, *args, **kwargs)

    return _wrapped

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\core\envfile.py ===
from pathlib import Path


def env_paths():
    paths = [
        Path("/etc/mohsen/mohsen.env"),
        Path("/opt/mohsen/mohsen.env"),
        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "mohsen.env",
    ]
    return [p for p in paths if p.is_file()]


def parse_env(path: Path) -> dict:
    data = {}
    for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        stripped = line.strip()
        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#") or "=" not in stripped:
            continue
        key, value = stripped.split("=", 1)
        data[key.strip()] = value.strip().strip('"').strip("'")
    return data


def load_env_into_os():
    import os

    for path in env_paths():
        for key, value in parse_env(path).items():
            os.environ.setdefault(key, value)
        return path
    return None


def write_env(path: Path, data: dict) -> None:
    lines = [f"{key}={value}" for key, value in data.items()]
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    try:
        path.chmod(0o640)
    except OSError:
        pass


def update_keys(changes: dict) -> list[str]:
    updated = []
    paths = env_paths()
    if not paths:
        raise FileNotFoundError("هیچ فایل mohsen.env پیدا نشد.")
    for path in paths:
        data = parse_env(path)
        data.update(changes)
        write_env(path, data)
        updated.append(str(path))
    return updated

from django.db import models


class ActivityLog(models.Model):
    username = models.CharField(max_length=150, blank=True)
    action = models.CharField(max_length=80)
    object_repr = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    message = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True)
    ip = models.CharField(max_length=64, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.action} {self.object_repr}"


class AdminDomain(models.Model):
    domain = models.CharField(max_length=253, unique=True)
    note = models.CharField(max_length=200, blank=True)
    https = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    is_primary = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-is_primary", "domain"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.domain

import io
import shutil
import tempfile
import zipfile

from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.test import TestCase, override_settings

from apps.categories.models import Category
from apps.core.bootstrap_data import ensure_categories, ensure_examples

from apps.sites.forms import WebsiteForm
from apps.sites.models import ContactMessage, Website
from mohsen_sdk import plugin_set


class ZipSafetyTests(TestCase):
    def test_rejects_parent_traversal(self):
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, "w") as archive:
            archive.writestr("../evil.txt", "nope")
        buf.seek(0)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(buf) as archive:
            with self.assertRaises(ZipRejected):
                inspect_zip(archive, ALLOWED_TEMPLATE_EXT)


@override_settings(MEDIA_ROOT=tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mohsen-test-"))
class PlatformTests(TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        from django.conf import settings

        shutil.rmtree(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, ignore_errors=True)
        super().tearDownClass()

    def setUp(self):
        ensure_categories()
        ensure_examples()
        get_user_model().objects.create_superuser("admin", "admin@example.com", "pass12345")

    def test_panel_requires_staff(self):
        response = self.client.get("/panel/")
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertIn("/panel/login/", response["Location"])
        self.client.login(username="admin", password="pass12345")
        response = self.client.get("/panel/")
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "پیشخوان")
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/panel/guide/template/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/panel/guide/plugin/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/panel/templates/").status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get("/panel/plugins/").status_code, 200)
        site = Website.objects.get(domain="zarrin.local")
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get(f"/panel/sites/{site.pk}/").status_code, 200)

    def test_gold_site_renders_and_silver_is_isolated(self):
        gold = self.client.get("/", HTTP_HOST="zarrin.local")
        self.assertEqual(gold.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(gold, "خانه زرین")
        silver = self.client.get("/", HTTP_HOST="hamrah.local")
        self.assertContains(silver, "همراه کالا")
        self.assertContains(silver, "لیست گوشی")

    def test_page_builder_escapes_html(self):
        site = Website.objects.get(domain="hamrah.local")
        plugin_set(
            site,
            "page-builder",
            "document",
            {
                "sections": [
                    {
                        "id": "a",
                        "type": "hero",
                        "title": "<script>alert(1)</script>",
                        "subtitle": "زیر",
                        "body": "",
                        "button_label": "",
                        "button_url": "",
                        "items": [],
                        "faqs": [],
                        "caps": [],
                    }
                ]
            },
        )
        response = self.client.get("/", HTTP_HOST="hamrah.local")
        body = response.content.decode()
        self.assertIn("&" + "lt;script&" + "gt;alert(1)", body)
        self.assertNotIn("<" + "script>alert(1)</" + "script>", body)

    def test_contact_form_notifies_sms_plugin_only_on_that_category(self):
        response = self.client.post(
            "/_mohsen/form/contact/",
            {"name": "علی", "phone": "09120000000", "message": "سلام از تست"},
            HTTP_HOST="zarrin.local",
        )
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.assertEqual(ContactMessage.objects.filter(website__domain="zarrin.local").count(), 1)
        from apps.plugins.models import PluginStorage

        log = PluginStorage.objects.get(website__domain="zarrin.local", plugin_slug="sms", key="log")
        self.assertTrue(log.value)
        self.assertFalse(
            PluginStorage.objects.filter(website__domain="hamrah.local", plugin_slug="sms").exists()
        )

    def test_template_must_match_category(self):
        gold = Category.objects.get(slug="gold")
        silver = Category.objects.get(slug="silver")
        phone = silver.templates.get(slug="silver-phones")
        form = WebsiteForm(
            data={
                "customer_name": "بد",
                "domain": "bad.example.com",
                "category": gold.pk,
                "template": phone.pk,
                "title": "بد",
                "status": "active",
                "content_json": "",
            }
        )
        self.assertFalse(form.is_valid())
        self.assertIn("template", form.errors)

    def test_preview_is_private(self):
        response = self.client.get("/_preview/zarrin.local/")
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 302)
        self.client.login(username="admin", password="pass12345")
        response = self.client.get("/_preview/zarrin.local/")
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertContains(response, "خانه زرین")

    def test_unknown_host_hides_panel_path(self):
        response = self.client.get("/", HTTP_HOST="unknown.example.com")
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertNotContains(response, "/panel/")
        self.assertContains(response, "محسن")

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib import messages
from django.contrib.auth import logout
from django.contrib.auth.forms import AuthenticationForm, PasswordChangeForm

from django.shortcuts import redirect, render

from apps.categories.models import Category

class PanelAuthForm(AuthenticationForm):
    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.fields["username"].label = "نام کاربری"
        self.fields["password"].label = "رمز عبور"


def login_view(request):
    if request.user.is_authenticated and request.user.is_staff:
        return redirect("dashboard")
    form = PanelAuthForm(request, data=request.POST or None)
    if request.method == "POST" and form.is_valid():
        user = form.get_user()
        if not user.is_staff:
            form.add_error(None, "این حساب به پنل دسترسی ندارد.")
        else:
            from django.contrib.auth import login

            login(request, user)
            log_action(request, "login", user.username, "ورود به پنل")
            return redirect("dashboard")
    return render(request, "panel/login.html", {"form": form})


@require_POST
def logout_view(request):
    if request.user.is_authenticated:
        log_action(request, "logout", request.user.username, "خروج")
    logout(request)
    return redirect("login")


@staff_required
def dashboard(request):
    return render(
        request,
        "panel/dashboard.html",
        {
            "counts": {
                "categories": Category.objects.count(),
                "templates": TemplatePackage.objects.count(),
                "plugins": PluginPackage.objects.count(),
                "websites": Website.objects.count(),
                "messages": ContactMessage.objects.count(),
            },
            "websites": Website.objects.select_related("category", "template")[:6],
            "activity": ActivityLog.objects.all()[:8],
            "messages_recent": ContactMessage.objects.select_related("website")[:6],
        },
    )

    return render(
        request,
        "panel/settings.html",
        {"domains": AdminDomain.objects.all(), "debug": settings.DEBUG},
    )

def activity_view(request):
    return render(request, "panel/activity.html", {"logs": ActivityLog.objects.all()[:200]})


@staff_required
def password_view(request):
    form = PasswordChangeForm(request.user, request.POST or None)
    form.fields["old_password"].label = "رمز فعلی"
    form.fields["new_password1"].label = "رمز جدید"
    form.fields["new_password2"].label = "تکرار رمز جدید"
    if request.method == "POST" and form.is_valid():
        user = form.save()
        update_session_auth_hash(request, user)
        log_action(request, "password", user.username, "تغییر رمز پنل")
        messages.success(request, "رمز عبور عوض شد.")
        return redirect("settings")
    return render(request, "panel/password.html", {"form": form})

    return render(request, "panel/guides/template.html", {"elements": ELEMENTS, "groups": GROUPS})

    return render(request, "panel/guides/plugin.html")

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\core\zipsafe.py ===
import stat
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings

ALLOWED_TEMPLATE_EXT = {
    ".html", ".css", ".js", ".json", ".svg", ".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg",
    ".webp", ".gif", ".ico", ".woff", ".woff2", ".txt", ".md", ".map", ".webmanifest",
}
ALLOWED_PLUGIN_EXT = ALLOWED_TEMPLATE_EXT | {".py"}


class ZipRejected(Exception):
    pass


def _is_ignored(name: str) -> bool:
    parts = Path(name).parts
    if not parts:
        return True
    if parts[0] == "__MACOSX" or name.startswith("__MACOSX/"):
        return True
    if Path(name).name in {".DS_Store", "Thumbs.db"}:
        return True
    return False


def _is_symlink(info: zipfile.ZipInfo) -> bool:
    mode = (info.external_attr >> 16) & 0xFFFF
    return bool(mode and stat.S_ISLNK(mode))


def inspect_zip(zf: zipfile.ZipFile, allowed_ext: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    bad = zf.testzip()
    if bad is not None:
        raise ZipRejected(f"فایل زیپ خراب است: {bad}")
    total = 0
    names = []
    for info in zf.infolist():
        name = info.filename.replace("\\", "/").lstrip("/")
        if _is_ignored(name):
            continue
        parts = Path(name).parts
        if ".." in parts or name.startswith("/"):
            raise ZipRejected("مسیر ناامن داخل زیپ رد شد.")
        if _is_symlink(info):
            raise ZipRejected("لینک نمادین داخل زیپ مجاز نیست.")
        total += info.file_size
        if info.file_size > settings.MOHSEN_MAX_UNCOMPRESSED or total > settings.MOHSEN_MAX_UNCOMPRESSED:
            raise ZipRejected("حجم غیرفشرده زیپ بیش از حد مجاز است.")
        if info.is_dir():
            continue
        ext = Path(name).suffix.lower()
        if ext not in allowed_ext:
            raise ZipRejected(f"پسوند مجاز نیست: {name}")
        names.append(name)
    if not names:
        raise ZipRejected("زیپ هیچ فایل مجازی ندارد.")
    if len(names) > 2000:
        raise ZipRejected("تعداد فایل‌های زیپ بیش از حد است.")
    return names


def extract_zip(zf: zipfile.ZipFile, dest: Path) -> None:
    dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    dest_resolved = dest.resolve()
    for info in zf.infolist():
        name = info.filename.replace("\\", "/").lstrip("/")
        if _is_ignored(name) or info.is_dir():
            continue
        target = (dest / name).resolve()
        if not str(target).startswith(str(dest_resolved)):
            raise ZipRejected("مسیر ناامن هنگام استخراج.")
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with zf.open(info, "r") as src, open(target, "wb") as out:
            remaining = info.file_size
            while True:
                chunk = src.read(1024 * 256)
                if not chunk:
                    break
                remaining -= len(chunk)
                if remaining < -1024:
                    raise ZipRejected("اندازه فایل با هدر زیپ نمی‌خواند.")
                out.write(chunk)


def locate_root(dest: Path, marker: str) -> Path:
    if (dest / marker).is_file():
        return dest
    children = [p for p in dest.iterdir() if p.name != "__MACOSX"]
    dirs = [p for p in children if p.is_dir()]
    files = [p for p in children if p.is_file() and p.name != ".DS_Store"]
    if len(dirs) == 1 and not files and (dirs[0] / marker).is_file():
        return dirs[0]
    return dest

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\plugins\loader.py ===
import importlib.util
import logging
from pathlib import Path

from mohsen_sdk.plugin_base import BasePlugin

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_CLASS_CACHE = {}


def load_plugin_class(root: Path, manifest: dict):
    slug = manifest["slug"]
    entry = str(manifest.get("entry") or "plugin.py").replace("\\", "/")
    if entry.startswith("/") or ".." in Path(entry).parts:
        raise ImportError("مسیر ورود افزونه نامعتبر است.")
    entry_path = (root / entry).resolve()
    if not str(entry_path).startswith(str(root.resolve())) or not entry_path.is_file():
        raise ImportError("فایل ورود افزونه پیدا نشد.")
    class_name = manifest.get("class") or "Plugin"
    module_name = f"mohsen_loaded_{slug}"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(module_name, entry_path)
    if spec is None or spec.loader is None:
        raise ImportError("بارگذاری ماژول افزونه ممکن نیست.")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    cls = getattr(module, class_name, None)
    if cls is None or not isinstance(cls, type) or not issubclass(cls, BasePlugin):
        raise ImportError(f"کلاس {class_name} باید از BasePlugin ارث ببرد.")
    return cls


def get_plugin_instance(package):
    root = Path(package.extracted_path)
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise ImportError("پوشه افزونه روی دیسک نیست.")
    stamp = int(root.stat().st_mtime)
    key = (package.slug, package.version, stamp)
    cls = _CLASS_CACHE.get(key)
    if cls is None:
        cls = load_plugin_class(root, package.manifest or {})
        _CLASS_CACHE.clear()
        _CLASS_CACHE[key] = cls
    instance = cls()
    instance.slug = instance.slug or package.slug
    instance.name = instance.name or package.name
    return instance


def iter_site_plugins(website):
    from apps.plugins.models import CategoryPlugin

    rows = (
        CategoryPlugin.objects.filter(
            category=website.category,
            enabled=True,
            plugin__is_active=True,
        )
        .select_related("plugin")
    )
    for row in rows:
        try:
            yield row, get_plugin_instance(row.plugin)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("plugin load failed: %s", row.plugin.slug)

from django.db import models

from apps.categories.models import Category


class PluginPackage(models.Model):
    name = models.CharField("نام", max_length=160)
    slug = models.SlugField("نامک", max_length=80, unique=True)
    version = models.CharField("نسخه", max_length=40, default="1.0.0")
    author = models.CharField("سازنده", max_length=120, blank=True)
    description = models.TextField("توضیح", blank=True)
    archive = models.FileField("فایل زیپ", upload_to="plugin_archives/", blank=True)
    extracted_path = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True)
    manifest = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField("فعال", default=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["name"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name


class CategoryPlugin(models.Model):
    category = models.ForeignKey(
        Category, verbose_name="دسته", on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="category_plugins"
    )
    plugin = models.ForeignKey(
        PluginPackage, verbose_name="افزونه", on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="assignments"
    )
    enabled = models.BooleanField("فعال برای دسته", default=True)
    settings = models.JSONField("تنظیمات پیش‌فرض دسته", default=dict, blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["category", "plugin"], name="uniq_category_plugin"),
        ]
        ordering = ["plugin__name"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.plugin} → {self.category}"


class PluginStorage(models.Model):
    website = models.ForeignKey("sites.Website", on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="plugin_storage")
    plugin_slug = models.SlugField(max_length=80)
    key = models.CharField(max_length=80)
    value = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(fields=["website", "plugin_slug", "key"], name="uniq_plugin_storage"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.plugin_slug}:{self.key}@{self.website_id}"

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\plugins\services.py ===
import io
import json
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile

from apps.core.zipsafe import ALLOWED_PLUGIN_EXT, ZipRejected, extract_zip, inspect_zip, locate_root
from apps.plugins.loader import load_plugin_class
from apps.plugins.models import PluginPackage

SLUG_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$")


def read_plugin_manifest(root: Path) -> dict:
    path = root / "plugin.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise ZipRejected("plugin.json در ریشه زیپ افزونه پیدا نشد.")
    try:
        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise ZipRejected(f"plugin.json نامعتبر است: {exc}") from exc
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise ZipRejected("plugin.json باید شیء JSON باشد.")
    for key in ("name", "slug"):
        if not str(data.get(key) or "").strip():
            raise ZipRejected(f"فیلد الزامی وجود ندارد: {key}")
    slug = str(data["slug"]).strip()
    if not SLUG_RE.fullmatch(slug):
        raise ZipRejected("slug افزونه فقط حروف کوچک انگلیسی، عدد و خط تیره.")
    entry = str(data.get("entry") or "plugin.py").strip().replace("\\", "/")
    if entry.startswith("/") or ".." in Path(entry).parts or not entry.endswith(".py"):
        raise ZipRejected("entry افزونه باید یک فایل py نسبی باشد.")
    if not (root / entry).is_file():
        raise ZipRejected(f"فایل ورود پیدا نشد: {entry}")
    hooks = data.get("hooks") or []
    if not isinstance(hooks, list):
        raise ZipRejected("hooks باید آرایه باشد.")
    return {
        "name": str(data["name"])[:160],
        "slug": slug,
        "version": str(data.get("version") or "1.0.0")[:40],
        "author": str(data.get("author") or "")[:120],
        "description": str(data.get("description") or "")[:4000],
        "entry": entry,
        "class": str(data.get("class") or "Plugin")[:80],
        "hooks": [str(h)[:80] for h in hooks],
    }


def store_plugin(*, raw: bytes, filename: str = "plugin.zip") -> tuple[PluginPackage, bool]:
    if len(raw) > settings.MOHSEN_MAX_ZIP_BYTES:
        raise ZipRejected("حجم فایل زیپ بیش از حد مجاز است.")
    if len(raw) < 32 or not zipfile.is_zipfile(io.BytesIO(raw)):
        raise ZipRejected("فایل بارگذاری‌شده یک زیپ معتبر نیست.")
    media_root = Path(settings.MEDIA_ROOT)
    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(raw)) as zf:
        inspect_zip(zf, ALLOWED_PLUGIN_EXT)
        tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mohsen-plug-"))
        try:
            extract_zip(zf, tmp)
            root = locate_root(tmp, "plugin.json")
            manifest = read_plugin_manifest(root)
            load_plugin_class(root, manifest)
            slug = manifest["slug"]
            final_parent = media_root / "plugins"
            final_parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            staging = final_parent / f".{slug}.staging"
            final = final_parent / slug
            if staging.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(staging)
            shutil.copytree(root, staging)
            if final.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(final)
            staging.rename(final)
        except ZipRejected:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ZipRejected(f"افزونه قابل بارگذاری نیست: {exc}") from exc
        finally:
            shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)

    existed = PluginPackage.objects.filter(slug=manifest["slug"]).exists()
    obj, _created = PluginPackage.objects.update_or_create(
        slug=manifest["slug"],
        defaults={
            "name": manifest["name"],
            "version": manifest["version"],
            "author": manifest["author"],
            "description": manifest["description"],
            "extracted_path": str(final),
            "manifest": manifest,
            "is_active": True,
        },
    )
    obj.archive.save(f"{manifest['slug']}-{manifest['version']}.zip", ContentFile(raw), save=True)
    return obj, not existed
