import io
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.contrib import messages
from django.http import FileResponse, Http404
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render

from apps.categories.models import Category
from apps.core.audit import log_action
from apps.core.decorators import staff_required
from apps.core.zipsafe import ZipRejected
from apps.plugins.loader import get_plugin_instance
from apps.plugins.models import CategoryPlugin, PluginPackage
from apps.plugins.services import store_plugin


def _zip_directory(root: Path) -> bytes:
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        for path in root.rglob("*"):
            if path.is_file() and "__pycache__" not in path.parts and path.suffix != ".pyc":
                zf.write(path, path.relative_to(root).as_posix())
    return buf.getvalue()


@staff_required
def plugin_list(request):
    q = (request.GET.get("q") or "").strip()
    rows = PluginPackage.objects.all()
    if q:
        rows = rows.filter(name__icontains=q)
    return render(request, "panel/plugins/list.html", {"rows": rows, "q": q})


@staff_required
def plugin_upload(request):
    error = ""
    if request.method == "POST":
        upload = request.FILES.get("archive")
        if upload is None:
            error = "فایل زیپ افزونه را انتخاب کنید."
        else:
            try:
                obj, created = store_plugin(raw=upload.read(), filename=upload.name)
            except ZipRejected as exc:
                error = str(exc)
            else:
                log_action(request, "plugin.upload", obj.slug, "ایجاد" if created else "به‌روزرسانی")
                messages.success(request, "افزونه در مرکز افزونه ثبت شد." if created else "افزونه به‌روز شد.")
                return redirect("plugin-detail", pk=obj.pk)
    return render(request, "panel/plugins/form.html", {"error": error})


@staff_required
def plugin_detail(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(PluginPackage, pk=pk)
    categories = list(Category.objects.filter(is_active=True))
    assignments = {row.category_id: row for row in obj.assignments.select_related("category")}
    instance = None
    load_error = ""
    try:
        instance = get_plugin_instance(obj)
    except Exception as exc:
        load_error = str(exc)

    if request.method == "POST" and request.POST.get("form") == "assign":
        chosen = {int(x) for x in request.POST.getlist("categories") if str(x).isdigit()}
        for category in categories:
            if category.id in chosen:
                CategoryPlugin.objects.update_or_create(
                    category=category, plugin=obj, defaults={"enabled": True}
                )
            else:
                CategoryPlugin.objects.filter(category=category, plugin=obj).delete()
        log_action(request, "plugin.assign", obj.slug, f"{len(chosen)} دسته")
        messages.success(request, "افزونه روی دسته‌های انتخاب‌شده فعال شد. همه وب‌سایت‌های آن دسته‌ها این افزونه را دارند.")
        return redirect("plugin-detail", pk=pk)

    if request.method == "POST" and request.POST.get("_plugin") and instance is not None:
        category = Category.objects.filter(pk=request.POST.get("_category") or 0).first()
        assignment = assignments.get(getattr(category, "id", None))
        if category and assignment:
            try:
                instance.handle_category_post(request, category, assignment)
                log_action(request, "plugin.category-settings", obj.slug, category.slug)
                messages.success(request, "تنظیمات پیش‌فرض دسته ذخیره شد.")
            except Exception as exc:
                messages.error(request, f"ذخیره تنظیمات افزونه ناموفق بود: {exc}")
        return redirect("plugin-detail", pk=pk)

    panels = []
    if instance is not None:
        for category in categories:
            assignment = assignments.get(category.id)
            if not assignment:
                continue
            try:
                panel = instance.category_admin_panel(request, category, assignment)
            except Exception as exc:
                panel = {"title": category.name, "html": f"<p>خطای پنل افزونه: {exc}</p>"}
            if panel:
                panels.append(panel)

    root = Path(obj.extracted_path)
    files = []
    if root.is_dir():
        files = sorted(p.relative_to(root).as_posix() for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file())[:300]
    return render(
        request,
        "panel/plugins/detail.html",
        {
            "obj": obj,
            "categories": categories,
            "assigned_ids": set(assignments),
            "panels": panels,
            "files": files,
            "load_error": load_error,
        },
    )


@staff_required
def plugin_toggle(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(PluginPackage, pk=pk)
    if request.method == "POST":
        obj.is_active = not obj.is_active
        obj.save(update_fields=["is_active", "updated_at"])
        log_action(request, "plugin.toggle", obj.slug, "فعال" if obj.is_active else "غیرفعال")
        messages.success(request, "وضعیت افزونه عوض شد.")
    return redirect("plugin-detail", pk=pk)


@staff_required
def plugin_delete(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(PluginPackage, pk=pk)
    if request.method == "POST":
        slug = obj.slug
        root = Path(obj.extracted_path)
        obj.delete()
        if root.is_dir() and root.parent.name == "plugins":
            import shutil

            shutil.rmtree(root, ignore_errors=True)
        log_action(request, "plugin.delete", slug, "")
        messages.success(request, "افزونه حذف شد.")
        return redirect("plugin-list")
    return render(request, "panel/confirm_delete.html", {"object_name": obj.name, "cancel_url_name": "plugin-detail", "cancel_pk": pk})


@staff_required
def example_plugin_download(request, name):
    from django.conf import settings as dj_settings

    mapping = {"sms": "sms", "page-builder": "page-builder"}
    folder = mapping.get(name)
    if not folder:
        raise Http404()
    root = Path(dj_settings.BASE_DIR) / "examples" / "plugins" / folder
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise Http404()
    return FileResponse(io.BytesIO(_zip_directory(root)), as_attachment=True, filename=f"{folder}.zip")

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\sites\domains.py ===
import re

HOST_RE = re.compile(
    r"^(?=.{1,253}$)(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z0-9-]{2,63}$"
)
LOCAL_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:localhost|(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+local)$")
IPV4_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}$")


def normalize_domain(value: str) -> str:
    raw = (value or "").strip().lower()
    raw = re.sub(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*://", "", raw)
    raw = raw.split("/")[0].split("?")[0].split("#")[0]
    if raw.startswith("["):
        raise ValueError("IPv6 به‌عنوان دامنه سایت پشتیبانی نمی‌شود.")
    if ":" in raw:
        raw = raw.split(":", 1)[0]
    raw = raw.strip(".")
    if raw.startswith("www.") and raw.count(".") >= 2:
        # Keep the value the operator typed, including www, after basic cleanup only.
        pass
    return raw


def validate_customer_domain(value: str) -> str:
    domain = normalize_domain(value)
    if not domain or " " in domain or ".." in domain:
        raise ValueError("دامنه نامعتبر است.")
    if IPV4_RE.fullmatch(domain):
        raise ValueError("برای سایت مشتری یک نام دامنه وارد کنید، نه آی‌پی.")
    if not (HOST_RE.fullmatch(domain) or LOCAL_RE.fullmatch(domain)):
        raise ValueError("دامنه باید شبیه example.com یا shop.example.com باشد.")
    return domain


def validate_admin_domain(value: str) -> str:
    domain = normalize_domain(value)
    if not domain:
        raise ValueError("دامنه خالی است.")
    if IPV4_RE.fullmatch(domain) or domain == "localhost" or HOST_RE.fullmatch(domain) or LOCAL_RE.fullmatch(domain):
        return domain
    raise ValueError("دامنه پنل نامعتبر است.")


def host_lookup_variants(host: str) -> list[str]:
    host = (host or "").split(":")[0].strip().lower().strip(".")
    if not host:
        return []
    variants = [host]
    if host.startswith("www."):
        variants.append(host[4:])
    else:
        variants.append("www." + host)
    seen = []
    for item in variants:
        if item and item not in seen:
            seen.append(item)
    return seen

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\sites\forms.py ===
import json
import re

from django import forms

from apps.sites.domains import validate_customer_domain
from apps.sites.models import Website
from apps.templates_engine.models import TemplatePackage

HEX_RE = re.compile(r"^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$")


class WebsiteForm(forms.ModelForm):
    content_json = forms.CharField(
        label="محتوای اختصاصی (JSON)",
        required=False,
        widget=forms.Textarea(attrs={"rows": 8, "dir": "ltr", "spellcheck": "false"}),
        help_text="جایگزین کلیدهای demo_data قالب می‌شود. مثال: {\"products\": []}. خالی یعنی داده نمونه قالب.",
    )

    class Meta:
        model = Website
        fields = [
            "customer_name", "domain", "category", "template", "title", "tagline",
            "description", "phone", "email", "address", "logo_text", "accent_color",
            "status", "notes",
        ]
        widgets = {
            "description": forms.Textarea(attrs={"rows": 3}),
            "notes": forms.Textarea(attrs={"rows": 3}),
            "accent_color": forms.TextInput(attrs={"type": "color"}),
        }

    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.fields["template"].queryset = TemplatePackage.objects.filter(is_active=True).select_related("category")
        self.fields["category"].queryset = self.fields["category"].queryset.filter(is_active=True)
        content = {}
        if self.instance.pk:
            content = (self.instance.settings or {}).get("content") or {}
        if content:
            self.fields["content_json"].initial = json.dumps(content, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        if not self.instance.pk and not self.initial.get("status"):
            self.initial["status"] = Website.Status.ACTIVE

    def clean_domain(self):
        try:
            domain = validate_customer_domain(self.cleaned_data.get("domain") or "")
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise forms.ValidationError(str(exc)) from exc
        qs = Website.objects.filter(domain=domain)
        if self.instance.pk:
            qs = qs.exclude(pk=self.instance.pk)
        if qs.exists():
            raise forms.ValidationError("این دامنه قبلاً ثبت شده است.")
        return domain

    def clean_accent_color(self):
        value = (self.cleaned_data.get("accent_color") or "").strip()
        if not value:
            return ""
        if not HEX_RE.fullmatch(value):
            raise forms.ValidationError("رنگ نامعتبر است.")
        return value.upper()

    def clean(self):
        cleaned = super().clean()
        category = cleaned.get("category")
        template = cleaned.get("template")
        if category and template and template.category_id != category.id:
            self.add_error("template", "قالب باید متعلق به همان دسته سایت باشد.")
        raw = (cleaned.get("content_json") or "").strip()
        if not raw:
            cleaned["content"] = {}
            return cleaned
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(raw)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            self.add_error("content_json", f"JSON نامعتبر است: {exc}")
            return cleaned
        if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
            self.add_error("content_json", "محتوا باید یک شیء JSON باشد.")
            return cleaned
        cleaned["content"] = parsed
        return cleaned

    def save(self, commit=True):
        obj = super().save(commit=False)
        settings_data = dict(obj.settings or {})
        settings_data["content"] = self.cleaned_data.get("content") or {}
        obj.settings = settings_data
        if not obj.logo_text:
            obj.logo_text = obj.title[:80]
        if commit:
            obj.save()
        return obj

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\sites\middleware.py ===
from apps.sites.domains import host_lookup_variants
from apps.sites.models import Website


class TenantMiddleware:
    def __init__(self, get_response):
        self.get_response = get_response

    def __call__(self, request):
        request.tenant = None
        request.url_prefix = ""
        host = request.get_host().split(":")[0].lower()
        variants = host_lookup_variants(host)
        if variants:
            request.tenant = (
                Website.objects.filter(domain__in=variants)
                .select_related("category", "template")
                .first()
            )
        return self.get_response(request)

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\sites\models.py ===
from django.db import models

from apps.categories.models import Category
from apps.templates_engine.models import TemplatePackage


class Website(models.Model):
    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        DRAFT = "draft", "پیش‌نویس"
        ACTIVE = "active", "فعال"
        SUSPENDED = "suspended", "معلق"

    customer_name = models.CharField("نام مشتری", max_length=160)
    domain = models.CharField("دامنه", max_length=253, unique=True)
    category = models.ForeignKey(Category, verbose_name="دسته", on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="websites")
    template = models.ForeignKey(
        TemplatePackage, verbose_name="قالب", on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="websites"
    )
    title = models.CharField("عنوان سایت", max_length=180)
    tagline = models.CharField("شعار", max_length=240, blank=True)
    description = models.TextField("توضیح", blank=True)
    phone = models.CharField("تلفن", max_length=40, blank=True)
    email = models.EmailField("ایمیل", blank=True)
    address = models.CharField("نشانی", max_length=300, blank=True)
    logo_text = models.CharField("متن نشان", max_length=80, blank=True)
    accent_color = models.CharField("رنگ تأکید", max_length=7, blank=True)
    status = models.CharField("وضعیت", max_length=20, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.ACTIVE)
    settings = models.JSONField("تنظیمات محتوا", default=dict, blank=True)
    notes = models.TextField("یادداشت داخلی", blank=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} ({self.domain})"


class ContactMessage(models.Model):
    website = models.ForeignKey(Website, on_delete=models.CASCADE, related_name="messages")
    name = models.CharField(max_length=160, blank=True)
    phone = models.CharField(max_length=40, blank=True)
    email = models.CharField(max_length=180, blank=True)
    body = models.TextField()
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    meta = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["-created_at"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.website.domain}: {self.name}"

import logging

from django.conf import settings
from django.core.cache import cache
from django.http import Http404, HttpResponse, HttpResponseNotAllowed
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.views.decorators.csrf import csrf_protect

from apps.plugins.loader import iter_site_plugins
from apps.sites.models import ContactMessage, Website
from apps.sites.rendering import render_page, serve_asset

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def healthz(request):
    return HttpResponse("ok", content_type="text/plain")


def page_not_found(request, exception):
    return render(request, "public/platform_message.html", {"title": "پیدا نشد", "body": "این مسیر وجود ندارد."}, status=404)


def server_error(request):
    return render(request, "public/platform_message.html", {"title": "خطای سرور", "body": "درخواست انجام نشد."}, status=500)


def _dispatch(request, website, rest, url_prefix):
    rest = (rest or "").strip("/")
    request.tenant = website
    request.url_prefix = url_prefix
    if rest.startswith("assets/"):
        return serve_asset(website, rest[len("assets/"):])
    if rest.startswith("_mohsen/form/"):
        name = rest[len("_mohsen/form/"):].strip("/")
        return handle_form(request, website, name or "contact", url_prefix)
    if rest == "":
        page_slug = ""
    else:
        if "/" in rest:
            raise Http404()
        page_slug = rest
    return render_page(request, website, page_slug, url_prefix=url_prefix)


def front(request, rest=""):
    website = getattr(request, "tenant", None)
    if website is None:
        if (rest or "").strip("/") == "":
            return render(request, "public/unconfigured.html")
        return render(request, "public/platform_message.html", {"title": "دامنه ثبت نشده", "body": "برای این میزبان سایتی تعریف نشده است."}, status=404)
    return _dispatch(request, website, rest, "")


def preview(request, domain, rest=""):
    if not request.user.is_authenticated or not request.user.is_staff:
        return redirect(settings.LOGIN_URL)
    website = get_object_or_404(Website.objects.select_related("category", "template"), domain=domain)
    return _dispatch(request, website, rest, f"/_preview/{website.domain}")


@csrf_protect
def handle_form(request, website, name, url_prefix):
    if request.method != "POST":
        return HttpResponseNotAllowed(["POST"])
    if website.status != Website.Status.ACTIVE and not request.user.is_staff:
        return HttpResponse("سایت فعال نیست.", status=403)
    if request.POST.get("company_website"):
        return _form_redirect(url_prefix)
    ip = request.META.get("REMOTE_ADDR") or "-"
    cache_key = f"mohsen-form:{website.id}:{ip}"
    count = cache.get(cache_key, 0)
    if count >= 8:
        return HttpResponse("تعداد ارسال بیش از حد است. کمی بعد دوباره تلاش کنید.", status=429)
    cache.set(cache_key, count + 1, 600)
    data = {
        "name": (request.POST.get("name") or "").strip()[:160],
        "phone": (request.POST.get("phone") or "").strip()[:40],
        "email": (request.POST.get("email") or "").strip()[:180],
        "message": (request.POST.get("message") or "").strip()[:4000],
    }
    if not data["name"] or not data["message"]:
        return HttpResponse("نام و متن پیام لازم است.", status=400)
    ContactMessage.objects.create(
        website=website,
        name=data["name"],
        phone=data["phone"],
        email=data["email"],
        body=data["message"],
        meta={"form": name[:40]},
    )
    for _row, inst in iter_site_plugins(website):
        try:
            inst.on_form_submit(website, name, data)
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("plugin form hook failed")
    return _form_redirect(url_prefix)


def _form_redirect(url_prefix):
    prefix = (url_prefix or "").rstrip("/")
    target = f"{prefix}/contact/?sent=1" if prefix else "/contact/?sent=1"
    return redirect(target)

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\sites\rendering.py ===
import logging
from pathlib import Path

from django.http import Http404, HttpResponse
from django.shortcuts import render
from jinja2 import FileSystemLoader, TemplateError, Undefined
from jinja2.sandbox import SandboxedEnvironment
from markupsafe import Markup

from apps.plugins.loader import iter_site_plugins

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class SilentUndefined(Undefined):
    def _fail_with_undefined_error(self, *args, **kwargs):
        return ""

    def __str__(self):
        return ""

    def __iter__(self):
        return iter(())

    def __bool__(self):
        return False


def site_payload(website):
    return {
        "id": website.id,
        "customer_name": website.customer_name,
        "domain": website.domain,
        "title": website.title,
        "tagline": website.tagline,
        "description": website.description,
        "phone": website.phone,
        "email": website.email,
        "address": website.address,
        "logo_text": website.logo_text or website.title,
        "accent_color": website.accent_color or website.category.color,
        "category": website.category.name,
        "category_slug": website.category.slug,
        "status": website.status,
    }


def merged_demo_data(website):
    manifest = website.template.manifest or {}
    base = dict(manifest.get("demo_data") or {})
    override = (website.settings or {}).get("content") or {}
    if isinstance(override, dict):
        base.update(override)
    return base


def build_environment(root: Path, url_prefix: str, website=None):
    prefix = url_prefix.rstrip("/")

    def asset(path=""):
        rel = str(path or "").lstrip("/")
        return f"{prefix}/assets/{rel}"

    def page_url(slug=""):
        slug = str(slug or "").strip("/")
        home = ""
        if not slug or slug == "home":
            return f"{prefix}/" or "/"
        return f"{prefix}/{slug}/"

    def form_action(name="contact"):
        name = str(name or "contact").strip("/")
        return f"{prefix}/_mohsen/form/{name}/"

    def memar(uid, key, default=""):
        return _memar_value(website, root, uid, key, default)

    env = SandboxedEnvironment(
        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(root)),
        autoescape=True,
        undefined=SilentUndefined,
    )
    env.globals["asset"] = asset
    env.globals["page_url"] = page_url
    env.globals["form_action"] = form_action
    env.globals["memar"] = memar
    return env, page_url


def _memar_value(website, root: Path, uid, key, default=""):
    from apps.architect.document import load_architect

    uid = str(uid or "")
    key = str(key or "")
    overrides = {}
    if website is not None:
        raw = (website.settings or {}).get("memar") or {}
        if isinstance(raw, dict):
            node_over = raw.get(uid) or {}
            if isinstance(node_over, dict):
                overrides = node_over
    value = overrides.get(key)
    if value not in (None, ""):
        return value
    document = load_architect(root)
    for node in document.get("nodes") or []:
        if isinstance(node, dict) and node.get("uid") == uid:
            settings = node.get("settings") or {}
            if isinstance(settings, dict) and settings.get(key) not in (None, ""):
                return settings.get(key)
            break
    return default if default is not None else ""


def plugin_contexts(website, request):
    bag = {}
    heads = []
    for _row, inst in iter_site_plugins(website):
        try:
            ctx = inst.site_context(website, request) or {}
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("site_context failed for %s", getattr(inst, "slug", "?"))
            ctx = {}
        slug = getattr(inst, "slug", "") or ""
        if slug:
            bag[slug] = ctx
            bag[slug.replace("-", "_")] = ctx
        try:
            head = inst.head_html(website, request) or ""
        except Exception:
            logger.exception("head_html failed")
            head = ""
        if head:
            heads.append(str(head))
    return bag, Markup("\n".join(heads))


def render_page(request, website, page_slug, url_prefix=""):
    if website.status == website.Status.SUSPENDED:
        return render(request, "public/suspended.html", {"website": website}, status=423)
    if website.status == website.Status.DRAFT and not request.user.is_staff:
        return render(request, "public/suspended.html", {"website": website, "draft": True}, status=403)
    template = website.template
    if not template.is_active:
        return render(request, "public/platform_message.html", {"title": "قالب غیرفعال است", "body": "قالب این سایت توسط مدیر غیرفعال شده است."}, status=503)
    root = Path(template.extracted_path)
    manifest = template.manifest or {}
    if not root.is_dir() or not (root / "template.json").is_file():
        return render(request, "public/platform_message.html", {"title": "قالب پیدا نشد", "body": "فایل‌های قالب روی سرور موجود نیست."}, status=500)
    pages = list(manifest.get("pages") or [])
    home = manifest.get("homepage") or (pages[0]["slug"] if pages else "home")
    if not page_slug:
        page_slug = home
    page = next((item for item in pages if item.get("slug") == page_slug), None)
    if page is None:
        raise Http404("صفحه در قالب تعریف نشده است.")
    env, page_url = build_environment(root, url_prefix, website)
    # Fix page_url so the manifest homepage also maps to root, not only "home".
    prefix = url_prefix.rstrip("/")

    def smart_page_url(slug=""):
        slug = str(slug or "").strip("/")
        if not slug or slug == home:
            return f"{prefix}/" if prefix else "/"
        return f"{prefix}/{slug}/"

    env.globals["page_url"] = smart_page_url
    plugins, head_html = plugin_contexts(website, request)
    context = {
        "site": site_payload(website),
        "page": {"slug": page["slug"], "title": page.get("title") or "", "file": page.get("file") or ""},
        "pages": pages,
        "data": merged_demo_data(website),
        "plugins": plugins,
        "plugins_head": head_html,
        "csrf_token": request.META.get("CSRF_COOKIE") or "",
        "sent": request.GET.get("sent") == "1",
        "url_prefix": prefix,
    }
    # Ensure a csrf cookie exists for public forms.
    from django.middleware.csrf import get_token

    context["csrf_token"] = get_token(request)
    try:
        html = env.get_template(page["file"]).render(context)
    except TemplateError as exc:
        logger.exception("template render failed for %s", website.domain)
        return render(
            request,
            "public/platform_message.html",
            {"title": "خطای قالب", "body": "قالب این صفحه خطا دارد. جزئیات در گزارش سرور ثبت شد.", "detail": str(exc) if request.user.is_staff else ""},
            status=500,
        )
    return HttpResponse(html)


def serve_asset(website, relative: str):
    from django.utils._os import safe_join

    rel = (relative or "").lstrip("/")
    if not rel or ".." in Path(rel).parts:
        raise Http404()
    root = Path(website.template.extracted_path) / "assets"
    try:
        path = Path(safe_join(str(root), rel))
    except Exception as exc:
        raise Http404() from exc
    if not path.is_file():
        raise Http404()
    content_types = {
        ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
        ".js": "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
        ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
        ".png": "image/png",
        ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
        ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
        ".webp": "image/webp",
        ".gif": "image/gif",
        ".ico": "image/x-icon",
        ".woff": "font/woff",
        ".woff2": "font/woff2",
        ".json": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
        ".txt": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
        ".webmanifest": "application/manifest+json",
    }
    data = path.read_bytes()
    response = HttpResponse(data, content_type=content_types.get(path.suffix.lower(), "application/octet-stream"))
    response["Cache-Control"] = "public, max-age=3600"
    return response

from django.contrib import messages
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render
from django.views.decorators.http import require_POST

from apps.core.audit import log_action
from apps.core.decorators import staff_required
from apps.plugins.loader import iter_site_plugins
from apps.sites.forms import WebsiteForm
from apps.sites.models import ContactMessage, Website
from apps.templates_engine.models import TemplatePackage
from apps.tls.models import Certificate
from apps.tls.service import enqueue, enqueue_remove


def _template_map():
    rows = TemplatePackage.objects.filter(is_active=True).values("id", "name", "category_id", "version")
    return list(rows)


def _schedule_site_tls(obj, *, previous_domain, previous_status, created):
    if previous_domain and previous_domain != obj.domain:
        enqueue_remove(previous_domain)
    became_active = previous_status != Website.Status.ACTIVE and obj.status == Website.Status.ACTIVE
    domain_changed = bool(previous_domain) and previous_domain != obj.domain
    if obj.status != Website.Status.ACTIVE:
        return ""
    if not (created or domain_changed or became_active):
        return ""
    return enqueue(obj.domain, Certificate.Kind.WEBSITE, website_id=obj.pk, force=False)


@staff_required
def site_list(request):
    q = (request.GET.get("q") or "").strip()
    rows = Website.objects.select_related("category", "template")
    if q:
        rows = rows.filter(domain__icontains=q) | rows.filter(customer_name__icontains=q) | rows.filter(title__icontains=q)
        rows = rows.distinct()
    rows = list(rows)
    tls_map = {cert.domain: cert for cert in Certificate.objects.filter(domain__in=[row.domain for row in rows])}
    return render(request, "panel/sites/list.html", {"rows": rows, "q": q, "tls_map": tls_map})


@staff_required
def site_create(request):
    form = WebsiteForm(request.POST or None)
    if request.method == "POST" and form.is_valid():
        obj = form.save()
        log_action(request, "site.create", obj.domain, obj.customer_name)
        messages.success(request, "سایت ثبت شد. با اشاره دادن DNS دامنه به این سرور، سایت باز می‌شود.")
        note = _schedule_site_tls(obj, previous_domain="", previous_status="", created=True)
        if note:
            messages.info(request, note)
        return redirect("site-edit", pk=obj.pk)
    return render(request, "panel/sites/form.html", {"form": form, "mode": "create", "template_map": _template_map()})


@staff_required
def site_edit(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(Website.objects.select_related("category", "template"), pk=pk)
    if request.method == "POST" and request.POST.get("_plugin"):
        slug = request.POST.get("_plugin")
        matched = None
        for _row, inst in iter_site_plugins(obj):
            if inst.slug == slug:
                matched = inst
                break
        if matched is None:
            messages.error(request, "این افزونه روی دسته سایت فعال نیست.")
        else:
            try:
                matched.handle_admin_post(request, obj)
                log_action(request, "site.plugin", obj.domain, slug)
                messages.success(request, "تغییر افزونه ذخیره شد.")
            except Exception as exc:
                messages.error(request, f"افزونه خطا داد: {exc}")
        return redirect("site-edit", pk=pk)

    form = WebsiteForm(request.POST or None, instance=obj)
    if request.method == "POST" and form.is_valid():
        previous_domain = obj.domain
        previous_status = obj.status
        saved = form.save()
        log_action(request, "site.update", saved.domain, saved.title)
        messages.success(request, "سایت به‌روز شد.")
        note = _schedule_site_tls(
            saved,
            previous_domain=previous_domain,
            previous_status=previous_status,
            created=False,
        )
        if note:
            messages.info(request, note)
        return redirect("site-edit", pk=pk)

    panels = []
    for _row, inst in iter_site_plugins(obj):
        try:
            panel = inst.admin_panel(request, obj)
        except Exception as exc:
            panel = {"title": inst.name or inst.slug, "html": f"<p>خطا در پنل افزونه: {exc}</p>"}
        if panel:
            panels.append(panel)
    inbox = obj.messages.all()[:30]
    tls_cert = Certificate.objects.filter(domain=obj.domain).first()
    return render(
        request,
        "panel/sites/form.html",
        {
            "form": form,
            "mode": "edit",
            "obj": obj,
            "template_map": _template_map(),
            "panels": panels,
            "inbox": inbox,
            "tls_cert": tls_cert,
        },
    )


@staff_required
def site_delete(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(Website, pk=pk)
    if request.method == "POST":
        domain = obj.domain
        enqueue_remove(domain)
        obj.delete()
        log_action(request, "site.delete", domain, "")
        messages.success(request, "سایت حذف شد.")
        return redirect("site-list")
    return render(request, "panel/confirm_delete.html", {"object_name": obj.domain, "cancel_url_name": "site-edit", "cancel_pk": pk})


@staff_required
@require_POST
def site_tls(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(Website, pk=pk)
    log_action(request, "tls.issue", obj.domain, "website")
    messages.info(request, enqueue(obj.domain, Certificate.Kind.WEBSITE, website_id=obj.pk, force=True))
    return redirect("site-edit", pk=pk)


@staff_required
def message_delete(request, pk):
    msg = get_object_or_404(ContactMessage, pk=pk)
    site_id = msg.website_id
    if request.method == "POST":
        msg.delete()
        messages.success(request, "پیام حذف شد.")
    return redirect("site-edit", pk=site_id)

from django.db import models

from apps.categories.models import Category


class TemplatePackage(models.Model):
    category = models.ForeignKey(Category, verbose_name="دسته", on_delete=models.PROTECT, related_name="templates")
    name = models.CharField("نام", max_length=160)
    slug = models.SlugField("نامک", max_length=80, unique=True)
    version = models.CharField("نسخه", max_length=40, default="1.0.0")
    author = models.CharField("سازنده", max_length=120, blank=True)
    description = models.TextField("توضیح", blank=True)
    archive = models.FileField("فایل زیپ", upload_to="template_archives/", blank=True)
    extracted_path = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True)
    manifest = models.JSONField(default=dict, blank=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField("فعال", default=True)
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["category__sort_order", "name"]

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name

    @property
    def pages(self):
        return list((self.manifest or {}).get("pages") or [])

import io
import json
import re
import shutil
import tempfile
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile
from jinja2 import FileSystemLoader, TemplateSyntaxError
from jinja2.sandbox import SandboxedEnvironment

from apps.architect.document import validate_architect
from apps.core.zipsafe import ALLOWED_TEMPLATE_EXT, ZipRejected, extract_zip, inspect_zip, locate_root
from apps.templates_engine.models import TemplatePackage

SLUG_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$")


def read_template_manifest(root: Path) -> dict:
    path = root / "template.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise ZipRejected("template.json در ریشه زیپ پیدا نشد. اگر زیپ یک پوشه والد دارد، فایل باید داخل همان پوشه باشد.")
    try:
        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise ZipRejected(f"template.json نامعتبر است: {exc}") from exc
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        raise ZipRejected("template.json باید یک شیء JSON باشد.")
    for key in ("name", "slug", "pages"):
        if not data.get(key):
            raise ZipRejected(f"فیلد الزامی وجود ندارد: {key}")
    slug = str(data["slug"]).strip()
    if not SLUG_RE.fullmatch(slug):
        raise ZipRejected("slug قالب فقط حروف کوچک انگلیسی، عدد و خط تیره مجاز است.")
    pages = data["pages"]
    if not isinstance(pages, list) or not pages:
        raise ZipRejected("حداقل یک صفحه در pages لازم است.")
    seen = set()
    root_resolved = root.resolve()
    for index, page in enumerate(pages, start=1):
        if not isinstance(page, dict):
            raise ZipRejected(f"ساختار صفحه {index} نادرست است.")
        for key in ("slug", "title", "file"):
            if not str(page.get(key) or "").strip():
                raise ZipRejected(f"صفحه {index} فیلد {key} ندارد.")
        page_slug = str(page["slug"]).strip()
        if not SLUG_RE.fullmatch(page_slug):
            raise ZipRejected(f"slug صفحه نامعتبر است: {page_slug}")
        if page_slug in seen:
            raise ZipRejected(f"slug صفحه تکراری است: {page_slug}")
        seen.add(page_slug)
        rel = str(page["file"]).strip().replace("\\", "/")
        if rel.startswith("/") or ".." in Path(rel).parts or not rel.endswith(".html"):
            raise ZipRejected(f"مسیر صفحه نامعتبر است: {rel}")
        target = (root / rel).resolve()
        if not str(target).startswith(str(root_resolved)) or not target.is_file():
            raise ZipRejected(f"فایل صفحه پیدا نشد: {rel}")
        page["slug"] = page_slug
        page["title"] = str(page["title"])[:160]
        page["file"] = rel
        page["in_menu"] = bool(page.get("in_menu", True))
    home = str(data.get("homepage") or pages[0]["slug"])
    if home not in seen:
        raise ZipRejected("homepage باید یکی از slugهای صفحات باشد.")
    demo = data.get("demo_data", {})
    if demo is None:
        demo = {}
    if not isinstance(demo, dict):
        raise ZipRejected("demo_data باید شیء JSON باشد.")
    page_slugs = [item["slug"] for item in pages]
    page_files = {item["slug"]: item["file"] for item in pages}
    architect = validate_architect(root, page_slugs, page_files)
    return {
        "name": str(data["name"])[:160],
        "slug": slug,
        "version": str(data.get("version") or "1.0.0")[:40],
        "author": str(data.get("author") or "")[:120],
        "description": str(data.get("description") or "")[:4000],
        "homepage": home,
        "pages": pages,
        "demo_data": demo,
        "engine": "jinja2",
        "architect": architect,
    }


def compile_template_tree(root: Path, manifest: dict) -> None:
    env = SandboxedEnvironment(loader=FileSystemLoader(str(root)), autoescape=True)
    try:
        for page in manifest["pages"]:
            env.get_template(page["file"])
    except TemplateSyntaxError as exc:
        where = exc.filename or page.get("file") or ""
        raise ZipRejected(f"خطای نحوی قالب در {where} خط {exc.lineno}: {exc.message}") from exc


def store_template(*, raw: bytes, category, filename: str = "template.zip") -> tuple[TemplatePackage, bool]:
    if len(raw) > settings.MOHSEN_MAX_ZIP_BYTES:
        raise ZipRejected("حجم فایل زیپ بیش از حد مجاز است.")
    if len(raw) < 32 or not zipfile.is_zipfile(io.BytesIO(raw)):
        raise ZipRejected("فایل بارگذاری‌شده یک زیپ معتبر نیست.")
    media_root = Path(settings.MEDIA_ROOT)
    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(raw)) as zf:
        inspect_zip(zf, ALLOWED_TEMPLATE_EXT)
        tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="mohsen-tpl-"))
        try:
            extract_zip(zf, tmp)
            root = locate_root(tmp, "template.json")
            manifest = read_template_manifest(root)
            compile_template_tree(root, manifest)
            slug = manifest["slug"]
            final_parent = media_root / "templates"
            final_parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            staging = final_parent / f".{slug}.staging"
            final = final_parent / slug
            if staging.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(staging)
            shutil.copytree(root, staging)
            if final.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(final)
            staging.rename(final)
        finally:
            shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)

    existed = TemplatePackage.objects.filter(slug=manifest["slug"]).exists()
    obj, _created = TemplatePackage.objects.update_or_create(
        slug=manifest["slug"],
        defaults={
            "category": category,
            "name": manifest["name"],
            "version": manifest["version"],
            "author": manifest["author"],
            "description": manifest["description"],
            "extracted_path": str(final),
            "manifest": manifest,
            "is_active": True,
        },
    )
    safe_name = f"{manifest['slug']}-{manifest['version']}.zip"
    obj.archive.save(safe_name, ContentFile(raw), save=True)
    return obj, not existed

import io
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

from django.contrib import messages
from django.http import FileResponse, Http404
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404, redirect, render

from apps.categories.models import Category
from apps.core.audit import log_action
from apps.core.decorators import staff_required
from apps.core.zipsafe import ZipRejected
from apps.templates_engine.models import TemplatePackage
from apps.templates_engine.services import store_template


def _zip_directory(root: Path) -> bytes:
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        for path in root.rglob("*"):
            if path.is_file() and "__pycache__" not in path.parts:
                zf.write(path, path.relative_to(root).as_posix())
    return buf.getvalue()


@staff_required
def template_list(request):
    q = (request.GET.get("q") or "").strip()
    rows = TemplatePackage.objects.select_related("category")
    if q:
        rows = rows.filter(name__icontains=q)
    return render(
        request,
        "panel/templates/list.html",
        {"rows": rows, "q": q, "categories": Category.objects.filter(is_active=True)},
    )


@staff_required
def template_upload(request):
    categories = Category.objects.filter(is_active=True)
    error = ""
    if request.method == "POST":
        category = categories.filter(pk=request.POST.get("category") or 0).first()
        upload = request.FILES.get("archive")
        if category is None:
            error = "دسته را انتخاب کنید."
        elif upload is None:
            error = "فایل زیپ را انتخاب کنید."
        else:
            try:
                obj, created = store_template(raw=upload.read(), category=category, filename=upload.name)
            except ZipRejected as exc:
                error = str(exc)
            else:
                log_action(request, "template.upload", obj.slug, "ایجاد" if created else "به‌روزرسانی")
                messages.success(request, "قالب ثبت شد." if created else "نسخه قالب به‌روز شد.")
                return redirect("template-detail", pk=obj.pk)
    return render(request, "panel/templates/form.html", {"categories": categories, "error": error})


@staff_required
def template_detail(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(TemplatePackage.objects.select_related("category"), pk=pk)
    root = Path(obj.extracted_path)
    files = []
    if root.is_dir():
        files = sorted(
            p.relative_to(root).as_posix()
            for p in root.rglob("*")
            if p.is_file()
        )[:400]
    return render(request, "panel/templates/detail.html", {"obj": obj, "files": files})


@staff_required
def template_toggle(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(TemplatePackage, pk=pk)
    if request.method == "POST":
        obj.is_active = not obj.is_active
        obj.save(update_fields=["is_active", "updated_at"])
        log_action(request, "template.toggle", obj.slug, "فعال" if obj.is_active else "غیرفعال")
        messages.success(request, "وضعیت قالب عوض شد.")
    return redirect("template-detail", pk=pk)


@staff_required
def template_delete(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(TemplatePackage, pk=pk)
    if request.method == "POST":
        if obj.websites.exists():
            messages.error(request, "این قالب روی حداقل یک سایت است و حذف نمی‌شود.")
            return redirect("template-detail", pk=pk)
        slug = obj.slug
        root = Path(obj.extracted_path)
        obj.delete()
        if root.is_dir() and root.parent.name == "templates":
            import shutil

            shutil.rmtree(root, ignore_errors=True)
        log_action(request, "template.delete", slug, "")
        messages.success(request, "قالب حذف شد.")
        return redirect("template-list")
    return render(request, "panel/confirm_delete.html", {"object_name": obj.name, "cancel_url_name": "template-detail", "cancel_pk": pk})


@staff_required
def template_download(request, pk):
    obj = get_object_or_404(TemplatePackage, pk=pk)
    if not obj.archive:
        raise Http404()
    return FileResponse(obj.archive.open("rb"), as_attachment=True, filename=f"{obj.slug}.zip")


@staff_required
def example_download(request, name):
    from django.conf import settings as dj_settings

    mapping = {
        "jewelry": "jewelry-atelier",
        "phones": "silver-phones",
    }
    folder = mapping.get(name)
    if not folder:
        raise Http404()
    root = Path(dj_settings.BASE_DIR) / "examples" / "templates" / folder
    if not root.is_dir():
        raise Http404()
    payload = _zip_directory(root)
    return FileResponse(io.BytesIO(payload), as_attachment=True, filename=f"{folder}.zip")

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand, CommandError

from apps.tls.service import perform_issue


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "صدور یا تمدید گواهی یک دامنه. روی سرور با root اجرا می‌شود."

    def add_arguments(self, parser):
        parser.add_argument("--domain", required=True)
        parser.add_argument("--force", action="store_true")

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        try:
            message = perform_issue(options["domain"], force=options["force"])
        except Exception as exc:
            raise CommandError(str(exc)) from exc
        self.stdout.write(message)

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\tls\acme.py ===
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings


class AcmeError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, message: str, output: str = ""):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.output = output


def acme_home() -> Path:
    return Path(getattr(settings, "TLS_ACME_HOME", "/root/.acme.sh"))


def acme_bin() -> Path:
    return acme_home() / "acme.sh"


def _run(args: list[str], timeout: int) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    binary = acme_bin()
    if not binary.is_file():
        raise AcmeError(
            "acme.sh نصب نیست. روی سرور sudo bash install.sh را دوباره اجرا کنید تا از get.acme.sh نصب شود."
        )
    cmd = [str(binary), "--home", str(acme_home()), *args]
    try:
        return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
        raise AcmeError("acme.sh بیش از حد طول کشید.", output=str(exc)) from exc
    except OSError as exc:
        raise AcmeError(f"اجرای acme.sh ممکن نشد: {exc}") from exc


def combined_output(result: subprocess.CompletedProcess) -> str:
    text = "\n".join(part for part in (result.stdout, result.stderr) if part)
    return text.strip()


def issue(domains: list[str], webroot: Path, email: str, *, force: bool = False) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    server = getattr(settings, "TLS_ACME_SERVER", "letsencrypt") or "letsencrypt"
    args = [
        "--issue",
        "--server",
        server,
        "--webroot",
        str(webroot),
        "--keylength",
        "2048",
        "--accountemail",
        email,
        "--dnssleep",
        "0",
    ]
    for domain in domains:
        args.extend(["-d", domain])
    if force:
        args.append("--force")
    return _run(args, timeout=240)


def install_cert(primary: str, dest: Path) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args = [
        "--install-cert",
        "-d",
        primary,
        "--cert-file",
        str(dest / "cert.pem"),
        "--key-file",
        str(dest / "privkey.pem"),
        "--fullchain-file",
        str(dest / "fullchain.pem"),
        "--ca-file",
        str(dest / "chain.pem"),
    ]
    return _run(args, timeout=60)


def remove(primary: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    return _run(["--remove", "-d", primary], timeout=60)


def cron() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    return _run(["--cron"], timeout=600)

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\tls\client.py ===
import json
import os
import socket


def socket_path() -> str:
    return os.environ.get("MOHSEN_TLS_SOCK", "/run/mohsen/tls.sock")


def notify(payload: dict, timeout: float = 2.0) -> dict:
    path = socket_path()
    data = (json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
    client = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    try:
        client.settimeout(timeout)
        client.connect(path)
        client.sendall(data)
        chunks = []
        while True:
            piece = client.recv(4096)
            if not piece:
                break
            chunks.append(piece)
            if b"\n" in piece:
                break
    finally:
        client.close()
    raw = b"".join(chunks).split(b"\n", 1)[0] or b"{}"
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return {"ok": False, "error": "پاسخ سرویس TLS نامعتبر بود."}
    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
        return {"ok": False, "error": "پاسخ سرویس TLS نامعتبر بود."}
    return parsed

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\tls\dns.py ===
"""
تشخیص DNS و آی‌پی سرور، با تحمل خطای شبکه‌های ایران.

سرویس‌دهنده‌های DNS سیستم (resolv.conf) در بسیاری از سرورهای ایرانی فقط
یک آدرس دارند که گاهی کند، فیلتر یا از کار افتاده است. اگر فقط به همان
resolver سیستمی تکیه کنیم، صدور TLS با خطای دروغین «رکورد DNS پیدا نشد»
متوقف می‌شود، در حالی که خود Let's Encrypt (که اعتبارسنجی واقعی را انجام
می‌دهد) هیچ مشکلی در تحریم یا فیلترینگ ندارد و دامنه را درست می‌بیند.

برای همین اینجا دو لایه داریم:
1) ریزالور سیستم (سریع‌ترین حالت، معمولاً کافی است).
2) اگر آن خالی برگشت: چند DNS resolver عمومی و معروفِ ایران (شکن،
   403online، الکترو) به‌علاوه چند resolver بین‌المللی، مستقیماً و موازی
   با UDP خام (بدون هیچ وابستگیِ بیرونی) پرسیده می‌شوند.

نتیجه این است که «رکورد پیدا نشد» فقط وقتی نشان داده می‌شود که دست‌کم
یکی از این resolverها واقعاً و قطعی (NOERROR/NXDOMAIN) جواب داده باشد؛
اگر همه timeout بزنند (یعنی وضعیت نامعلوم است، نه قطعاً خالی)، لایه‌ی
سرویس (service.py) به‌جایِ متوقف‌کردن کار، صدور acme.sh را هم امتحان
می‌کند -- چون داور نهایی، سرورهای اعتبارسنجیِ خودِ Let's Encrypt هستند
نه resolver محلی ما.
"""

import random
import re
import socket
import struct
import subprocess
import urllib.request
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed

IPV4_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}$")
IPV6_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f:]+$")

# resolverهای عمومی برای حالتی که resolver سیستم جواب نداد. چندتای اول
# resolverهای «ضد تحریم/ضد فیلتر» معروف ایران هستند که از داخل شبکه‌ی
# کشور معمولاً بدون مشکل در دسترس‌اند؛ بقیه برای تکمیل و تنوع‌اند.
FALLBACK_RESOLVERS = [
    "178.22.122.100",  # Shecan
    "185.51.200.2",    # Shecan
    "10.202.10.10",    # 403.online
    "10.202.10.11",    # 403.online
    "78.157.42.100",   # Electro
    "78.157.42.101",   # Electro
    "1.1.1.1",         # Cloudflare
    "1.0.0.1",
    "8.8.8.8",          # Google
    "8.8.4.4",
    "9.9.9.9",          # Quad9
]

# برای خودِ سرور هم چند سرویسِ تشخیص آی‌پی عمومی را امتحان می‌کنیم؛ اگر
# یکی از آن‌ها در دسترس نبود (فیلتر/قطعی) بقیه جایگزین می‌شوند.
IP_ECHO_URLS = [
    "https://api.ipify.org",
    "https://ipv4.icanhazip.com",
    "https://ifconfig.me/ip",
    "https://checkip.amazonaws.com",
]


def _valid_v4(value: str) -> bool:
    if not IPV4_RE.fullmatch(value or ""):
        return False
    parts = [int(part) for part in value.split(".")]
    if any(part > 255 for part in parts):
        return False
    if value.startswith("127.") or value == "0.0.0.0" or value.startswith("169.254."):
        return False
    return True


def _resolver_list() -> list[str]:
    extra: list[str] = []
    try:
        from django.conf import settings

        raw = getattr(settings, "TLS_DNS_RESOLVERS", "") or ""
        extra = [token.strip() for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", raw) if token.strip()]
    except Exception:
        extra = []
    merged = extra + [ip for ip in FALLBACK_RESOLVERS if ip not in extra]
    return merged


def _build_query(name: str, qtype: int) -> tuple[bytes, int]:
    tid = random.randint(0, 65535)
    header = struct.pack(">HHHHHH", tid, 0x0100, 1, 0, 0, 0)
    labels = [label for label in name.rstrip(".").split(".") if label]
    question = b""
    for label in labels:
        encoded = label.encode("idna") if not label.isascii() else label.encode("ascii")
        question += bytes([len(encoded)]) + encoded
    question += b"\x00" + struct.pack(">HH", qtype, 1)
    return header + question, tid


def _parse_name(data: bytes, offset: int) -> tuple[str, int]:
    labels = []
    hops = 0
    cursor = offset
    end_of_record = None
    while hops < 128:
        if cursor >= len(data):
            break
        length = data[cursor]
        if length == 0:
            cursor += 1
            if end_of_record is None:
                end_of_record = cursor
            break
        if length & 0xC0 == 0xC0:
            if cursor + 1 >= len(data):
                break
            pointer = ((length & 0x3F) << 8) | data[cursor + 1]
            if end_of_record is None:
                end_of_record = cursor + 2
            cursor = pointer
            hops += 1
            continue
        cursor += 1
        labels.append(data[cursor:cursor + length].decode("ascii", "ignore"))
        cursor += length
    if end_of_record is None:
        end_of_record = cursor
    return ".".join(labels), end_of_record


def _parse_response(data: bytes, tid: int) -> tuple[set[str], bool]:
    """برمی‌گرداند (آی‌پی‌های پیدا شده, آیا پاسخ قطعی/معتبر بود)."""
    if len(data) < 12:
        return set(), False
    r_tid, flags, qdcount, ancount = struct.unpack(">HHHH", data[0:8])
    if r_tid != tid:
        return set(), False
    is_response = bool(flags & 0x8000)
    rcode = flags & 0x000F
    if not is_response or rcode not in (0, 3):  # NOERROR یا NXDOMAIN معتبرند
        return set(), False
    offset = 12
    for _ in range(qdcount):
        _, offset = _parse_name(data, offset)
        offset += 4
    found: set[str] = set()
    for _ in range(ancount):
        if offset >= len(data):
            break
        _, offset = _parse_name(data, offset)
        if offset + 10 > len(data):
            break
        rtype, _rclass, _ttl, rdlength = struct.unpack(">HHIH", data[offset:offset + 10])
        offset += 10
        rdata = data[offset:offset + rdlength]
        offset += rdlength
        if rtype == 1 and len(rdata) == 4:
            found.add(".".join(str(b) for b in rdata))
        elif rtype == 28 and len(rdata) == 16:
            found.add(":".join(f"{rdata[i]:02x}{rdata[i + 1]:02x}" for i in range(0, 16, 2)))
    return found, True


def _query_one(resolver: str, host: str, qtype: int, timeout: float) -> tuple[set[str], bool]:
    try:
        packet, tid = _build_query(host, qtype)
    except UnicodeError:
        return set(), False
    sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
    try:
        sock.settimeout(timeout)
        sock.sendto(packet, (resolver, 53))
        data, _from = sock.recvfrom(4096)
    except OSError:
        return set(), False
    finally:
        sock.close()
    return _parse_response(data, tid)


def _query_fallback(host: str, timeout: float = 2.5, resolver_count: int = 6) -> tuple[set[str], int]:
    """چند resolver عمومی را مستقیماً و موازی می‌پرسد (بدون وابستگی به
    resolv.conf سیستم). برمی‌گرداند (آی‌پی‌ها, تعداد resolverهای متفاوتی
    که پاسخ معتبر داده‌اند)."""
    pool = _resolver_list()[:resolver_count]
    if not pool:
        return set(), 0
    found: set[str] = set()
    confirmed_resolvers: set[str] = set()
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max(len(pool) * 2, 1)) as executor:
        future_map = {}
        for resolver in pool:
            future_map[executor.submit(_query_one, resolver, host, 1, timeout)] = resolver  # A
            future_map[executor.submit(_query_one, resolver, host, 28, timeout)] = resolver  # AAAA
        for future in as_completed(future_map, timeout=timeout + 2):
            resolver = future_map[future]
            try:
                ips, ok = future.result()
            except Exception:
                continue
            if ok:
                confirmed_resolvers.add(resolver)
            found |= ips
    return found, len(confirmed_resolvers)


# برای این‌که یک resolver خراب/فیلترشده (مثلاً یک مسیر شبکه‌ای که هر پرس‌و‌جوی
# UDP:53 را با NXDOMAIN جعلی جواب می‌دهد) باعث خطای کاذب «رکورد نیست» نشود،
# فقط وقتی نتیجه را قطعی می‌دانیم که حداقل این تعداد منبعِ مستقل (resolver
# سیستم + resolverهای fallback) همگی روی «رکوردی نیست» توافق داشته باشند.
CONFIRM_QUORUM = 2


def resolve_ips(host: str) -> tuple[set[str], bool]:
    """آی‌پی‌های یک نام را برمی‌گرداند، به‌همراه این‌که نتیجه قطعی است یا نه.

    قطعی یعنی دست‌کم CONFIRM_QUORUM منبعِ مستقل پاسخ معتبر (حتی خالی/
    NXDOMAIN) داده‌اند. اگر این‌تعداد به توافق نرسیدند -- چه به‌خاطر timeout
    و چه به‌خاطر فیلترشدنِ مسیر به resolverها -- نتیجه نامعلوم است و نباید
    به‌معنای «رکورد وجود ندارد» گرفته شود.
    """
    found: set[str] = set()
    votes = 0
    try:
        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)
        votes += 1
    except socket.gaierror as exc:
        infos = []
        # EAI_NONAME/EAI_NODATA یعنی resolver سیستم واقعاً جواب داد و
        # رکوردی نیست (یک رأی قطعی). بقیه‌ی کدها (EAI_AGAIN موقت، EAI_FAIL
        # و...) یعنی خودِ resolver مشکل دارد -- این رأی حساب نمی‌شود.
        definite_codes = {
            code
            for code in (getattr(socket, "EAI_NONAME", None), getattr(socket, "EAI_NODATA", None))
            if code is not None
        }
        if exc.errno in definite_codes:
            votes += 1
    except OSError:
        infos = []
    for family, _type, _proto, _canon, sockaddr in infos:
        if family not in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6):
            continue
        ip = sockaddr[0].split("%", 1)[0].lower()
        if ip:
            found.add(ip)
    if found:
        return found, True

    fallback_ips, fallback_votes = _query_fallback(host)
    if fallback_ips:
        return {ip.lower() for ip in fallback_ips}, True
    votes += fallback_votes
    return set(), votes >= CONFIRM_QUORUM


def server_addresses() -> set[str]:
    """آی‌پی‌های این ماشین، به‌اضافه آی‌پی عمومی اگر پشت NAT باشد."""
    from apps.core.access import server_ips

    found = {ip for ip in server_ips() if _valid_v4(ip)}
    for url in IP_ECHO_URLS:
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=3) as response:
                public = (response.read() or b"").decode().strip()
            if _valid_v4(public):
                found.add(public)
                break
        except Exception:
            continue
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["ip", "-6", "-o", "addr", "show", "scope", "global"],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=3,
        )
        for match in re.findall(r"inet6\s+([0-9a-f:]+)", (result.stdout or "").lower()):
            if not match.startswith("fe80") and IPV6_RE.fullmatch(match):
                found.add(match)
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        pass
    return found


def points_here(host: str, addresses: set[str]) -> tuple[bool, set[str], bool]:
    """برمی‌گرداند (اشاره‌می‌کند؟, آی‌پی‌های پیدا شده, قطعی‌بودن نتیجه)."""
    resolved, confirmed = resolve_ips(host)
    if not resolved or not addresses:
        return False, resolved, confirmed
    return bool(resolved & addresses), resolved, confirmed

from django.db import models


class Certificate(models.Model):
    class Kind(models.TextChoices):
        PANEL = "panel", "پنل"
        WEBSITE = "website", "وب‌سایت"

    class Status(models.TextChoices):
        QUEUED = "queued", "در صف"
        ISSUING = "issuing", "در حال صدور"
        ACTIVE = "active", "فعال"
        FAILED = "failed", "ناموفق"

    domain = models.CharField("دامنه", max_length=253, unique=True)
    kind = models.CharField("نوع", max_length=20, choices=Kind.choices)
    status = models.CharField("وضعیت", max_length=20, choices=Status.choices, default=Status.QUEUED)
    sans = models.JSONField("نام‌های گواهی", default=list, blank=True)
    issuer = models.CharField("صادرکننده", max_length=80, default="Let's Encrypt", blank=True)
    issued_at = models.DateTimeField("زمان صدور", null=True, blank=True)
    expires_at = models.DateTimeField("انقضا", null=True, blank=True)
    last_attempt_at = models.DateTimeField("آخرین تلاش", null=True, blank=True)
    last_error = models.TextField("آخرین خطا", blank=True)
    auto = models.BooleanField("تمدید خودکار", default=True)
    website = models.ForeignKey(
        "sites.Website",
        verbose_name="وب‌سایت",
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        related_name="certificates",
    )
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        ordering = ["kind", "domain"]
        verbose_name = "گواهی"
        verbose_name_plural = "گواهی‌ها"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.domain} ({self.get_status_display()})"

import re

IPV4_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}$")
DOMAIN_RE = re.compile(
    r"^(?=.{1,253}$)(?:[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?\.)+[a-z0-9-]{2,63}$"
)
_SCHEME_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*://")


def clean_domain(value: str) -> str:
    raw = (value or "").strip().lower()
    raw = _SCHEME_RE.sub("", raw)
    raw = raw.split("/")[0].split("?")[0].split("#")[0]
    if raw.startswith("["):
        return raw
    if ":" in raw:
        raw = raw.split(":", 1)[0]
    return raw.strip(".")


def is_ip(value: str) -> bool:
    if not IPV4_RE.fullmatch(value or ""):
        return False
    return all(int(part) <= 255 for part in value.split("."))


def is_public_dns_name(value: str) -> bool:
    """نامی که Let's Encrypt با HTTP-01 می‌تواند برایش گواهی بدهد."""
    if not value or is_ip(value) or value in {"localhost", "localhost.localdomain"}:
        return False
    if value.endswith((".local", ".localhost", ".invalid", ".test", ".example", ".lan")):
        return False
    if ".." in value or value.startswith("-") or value.endswith("-"):
        return False
    return bool(DOMAIN_RE.fullmatch(value))


def partner_name(domain: str) -> str:
    if domain.startswith("www."):
        return domain[4:]
    return "www." + domain

=== FILE: F:\desktop\مستندات سایت ساز\جنگو\5\apps\tls\nginx.py ===
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

from django.conf import settings


def ssl_dir() -> Path:
    return Path(getattr(settings, "TLS_SSL_DIR", "/etc/mohsen/ssl"))


def nginx_group() -> str:
    import grp

    for name in ("www-data", "nginx"):
        try:
            grp.getgrnam(name)
            return name
        except KeyError:
            continue
    return "root"


def secure_tree(path: Path) -> None:
    """Workerهای nginx با گواهی متغیر باید خود فایل را بخوانند."""
    import grp

    group = nginx_group()
    try:
        gid = grp.getgrnam(group).gr_gid
    except KeyError:
        gid = 0
    if not path.exists():
        return
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(path):
        os.chown(root, 0, gid)
        os.chmod(root, 0o750)
        for name in dirs:
            child = Path(root) / name
            os.chown(child, 0, gid)
            os.chmod(child, 0o750)
        for name in files:
            file_path = Path(root) / name
            os.chown(file_path, 0, gid)
            os.chmod(file_path, 0o640)


def installed_certs() -> list[tuple[list[str], Path, Path]]:
    root = ssl_dir()
    found = []
    if not root.is_dir():
        return found
    for child in sorted(root.iterdir()):
        if not child.is_dir() or child.name.startswith(("_", ".")):
            continue
        fullchain = child / "fullchain.pem"
        key = child / "privkey.pem"
        if not (fullchain.is_file() and key.is_file()):
            continue
        names = [child.name]
        names_file = child / "names.txt"
        if names_file.is_file():
            parsed = []
            for line in names_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
                item = line.strip().lower()
                if item and item not in parsed:
                    parsed.append(item)
            if parsed:
                names = parsed
        found.append((names, fullchain.resolve(), key.resolve()))
    return found


def _render(found) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    cert_lines = []
    key_lines = []
    redirect_lines = []
    seen = set()
    for names, fullchain, key in found:
        for name in names:
            if name in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(name)
            cert_lines.append(f"{name} {fullchain};")
            key_lines.append(f"{name} {key};")
            redirect_lines.append(f"{name} 1;")
    return (
        "\n".join(cert_lines) + ("\n" if cert_lines else ""),
        "\n".join(key_lines) + ("\n" if key_lines else ""),
        "\n".join(redirect_lines) + ("\n" if redirect_lines else ""),
    )


def _nginx_test() -> tuple[bool, str]:
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(["nginx", "-t"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
        return False, str(exc)
    output = "\n".join(part for part in (result.stderr, result.stdout) if part).strip()
    return result.returncode == 0, output


def _nginx_reload() -> None:
    if shutil.which("systemctl"):
        subprocess.run(["systemctl", "reload", "nginx"], check=False, timeout=30)
        return
    subprocess.run(["nginx", "-s", "reload"], check=False, timeout=30)


def apply_maps() -> tuple[bool, str]:
    root = ssl_dir()
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cert_map, key_map, redirect_map = _render(installed_certs())
    targets = {
        "cert.map": cert_map,
        "key.map": key_map,
        "redirect.map": redirect_map,
    }
    backups = {}
    for name in targets:
        path = root / name
        backups[name] = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if path.is_file() else ""
        path.write_text(targets[name], encoding="utf-8")
        os.chmod(path, 0o644)
    ok, output = _nginx_test()
    if not ok:
        for name, content in backups.items():
            (root / name).write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
        _nginx_test()
        return False, output or "nginx -t ناموفق بود. نقشه گواهی برگردانده شد."
    try:
        _nginx_reload()
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
        return False, str(exc)
    return True, ""
